- **By hand:** Escalate; token verification on the grid has to wait.
- **Could break when:** curl replaced by a wrapper, or by a module version that parses config files differently.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:506-513,708-711; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:21-22

### `grid-login.verify-tokens` Grid-side token verification and its parser

Depends on: `grid-login.tokens-pushed`, `grid-login.https-egress-and-ca`, `grid-login.curl-config-stdin`

- **Assumes:** `ashley verify-tokens` prints one line per token in a fixed vocabulary. HF_TOKEN and GITHUB_TOKEN: 'ok (<name>)', 'rejected or unreachable' or 'missing'. CLAUDE_TOKEN: 'ok', 'rejected', 'present (HTTP <code>)' or 'missing'. `access requirements` and `ashley diag` grep these lines. For HF and GitHub, 'rejected' and 'unreachable' are one message, so a 429 or a network failure reads as a bad token.
- **Check:** On the login node: `~/.ashley/bin/ashley verify-tokens` prints three lines starting with HF_TOKEN, GITHUB_TOKEN and CLAUDE_TOKEN. Compare each with the laptop's validateToken() for the same token, which is an independent path. A disagreement points to the parser, egress or a rate limit, not to the token.
- **Looks like:** `no secrets.env`; `HF_TOKEN rejected or unreachable`; `GITHUB_TOKEN rejected or unreachable`; `Hugging Face token missing or rejected`; `GitHub token missing or rejected`
- **Repair:** When the laptop says the token is fine, retry once after 60 s (rate limit or a passing egress problem). Safe.
- **By hand:** Renew only a token that the laptop also rejects.
- **Could break when:** Vendors changing their JSON formatting breaks the grep parsing. 401 and network errors remain indistinguishable.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:575-590,720-726,789-813

### `grid-login.git-https-github` git on the login node can push to github.com over HTTPS

Depends on: `grid-login.https-egress-and-ca`

- **Assumes:** The login node's git can clone a bundle made by a modern git, use an in-memory credential helper (`!f() { echo username=x-access-token; echo password=$GITHUB_TOKEN; }; f`) and negotiate TLS with github.com. CentOS 7 ships git 1.8.3.1; the version on warrior was not recorded. A bearer token passed through http.extraheader was refused.
- **Check:** On the login node: `git --version`, then `git ls-remote https://github.com/git/git.git HEAD` prints one hash line (anonymous and cheap).
- **Looks like:** `fatal: unable to access 'https://github.com/...': Peer's Certificate issuer is not recognized.`; `error: RPC failed`; `SSL connect error`; `the bundle is refused as unsupported (a bundle version mismatch)`
- **Repair:** Run the push inside the worker container, which has Debian 12's git, with `srun --jobid <worker> --overlap` instead of on the login node. Safe.
- **By hand:** Load a newer git module if C&IT provides one (`module avail git`), or push from the laptop.
- **Could break when:** GitHub tightening its TLS, ciphers or protocol requirements beyond what an unpatched CentOS 7 git and libcurl can do; changes to the bundle format in modern git.
- **Code:** tools/push_via_grid.sh:12-15; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:151-157

### `grid-login.sshd-pubkey-batchmode` Login sshd accepts a public key alone (BatchMode, no OTP)

Depends on: `network.grid-login`, `laptop.openssh`, `laptop.ssh-key`, `laptop.ssh-config-block`, `grid-login.authorized-keys`

- **Assumes:** sshd on warrior accepts publickey authentication without a password or Authenticator code. Every Ashley call can then run with BatchMode=yes, ConnectTimeout=20, MACs=+hmac-sha1, AddressFamily=inet and GSSAPIAuthentication=no. The OTP is typed only once, to install the key.
- **Check:** laptop: `ssh -o BatchMode=yes -o ConnectTimeout=20 -o MACs=+hmac-sha1 -o AddressFamily=inet wsu-grid hostname`. Pass = exit 0 and 'warrior' within about 5 s.
- **Looks like:** `Permission denied (publickey,gssapi-keyex,gssapi-with-mic,keyboard-interactive).`; `no matching MAC found. Their offer: hmac-sha1`; `Connection timed out during banner exchange`; `kex_exchange_identification: read: Connection reset by peer`; `the grid does not accept the key yet`
- **Repair:** On a MAC or IPv6 symptom, rewrite the ssh config block (wizard step sshConfig). On a publickey refusal while the host answers, rerun wizard step installKey (one interactive password+OTP).
- **By hand:** Run the wizard's 'Install key' step again; check the key line ends with 'ashley' in ~/.ssh/authorized_keys.
- **Could break when:** High. If C&IT sets AuthenticationMethods publickey,keyboard-interactive (2FA on keys), every BatchMode call fails. Recovery would need one interactive login per day held open (Windows OpenSSH has no ControlMaster; the in-panel ssh2 client could hold it). Dropping hmac-sha1 on the server is harmless, since +hmac-sha1 only appends. A laptop OpenSSH that removes hmac-sha1 while the grid offers nothing else breaks every call.
- **Code:** extension/src/core/shell.ts:96-108; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:340-357; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:207-212; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:291

### `grid-login.authorized-keys` authorized_keys holds the ashley key with StrictModes-safe permissions

Depends on: `account.grid-2fa`, `laptop.ssh-key`

- **Assumes:** ~/.ssh/authorized_keys contains the ashley public key, and sshd StrictModes is satisfied: the home directory is not group- or world-writable, ~/.ssh is 700 and authorized_keys is 600, all owned by the user. The installKey command sets umask 077 for new files but never repairs an existing ~/.ssh.
- **Check:** login node (needs one working login): `stat -c '%a %U %n' ~ ~/.ssh ~/.ssh/authorized_keys; grep -c 'ashley$' ~/.ssh/authorized_keys`. Pass = home without g+w/o+w, 700, 600, count >= 1. laptop: BatchMode fails with publickey while the password path works = this node.
- **Looks like:** `Permission denied (publickey...) after the key step`; `Authentication refused: bad ownership or modes for directory (sshd log only, invisible to the student)`; `still prompted:`
- **Repair:** Over a working BatchMode session: `chmod go-w ~; chmod 700 ~/.ssh; chmod 600 ~/.ssh/authorized_keys`. Without one: rerun installKey with those chmods added to its remote command.
- **By hand:** In a grid terminal, run the three chmods and confirm the key line is present.
- **Could break when:** If C&IT moves keys to a central store (AuthorizedKeysCommand or SSSD sshPublicKey), ~/.ssh/authorized_keys is ignored.
- **Code:** extension/src/core/wizard.ts:340-357

### `grid-login.host-key-stable` Login host key unchanged since first contact

Depends on: `network.grid-login`, `laptop.openssh`

- **Assumes:** Every call runs with StrictHostKeyChecking and BatchMode, so the host key recorded at first contact (accept-new) must stay valid. The wsu-grid alias must resolve to hosts that present one key set.
- **Check:** laptop: `ssh-keygen -F warrior.grid.wayne.edu` finds an entry, and `ssh -o BatchMode=yes wsu-grid true` prints no host-key warning.
- **Looks like:** `WARNING: REMOTE HOST IDENTIFICATION HAS CHANGED!`; `Host key verification failed.`
- **By hand:** Check the new fingerprint against C&IT's published one (or ask hpc@wayne.edu), then `ssh-keygen -R warrior.grid.wayne.edu` and reconnect. Never accept a changed key automatically.
- **Could break when:** An EL7 to EL8/9 reinstall of the login node, or a second login node behind the same name, changes the host key for every student on the same day.
- **Code:** extension/src/core/wizard.ts:352; extension/src/core/sshConfig.ts:36

### `grid-login.bashrc-quiet` ~/.bashrc prints nothing for non-interactive ssh

Depends on: `account.grid-login-shell`

- **Assumes:** Red Hat bash sources ~/.bashrc for every non-interactive ssh command, so ~/.bashrc must write nothing to stdout. Output corrupts sftp/scp. It also lands before READY and JSON lines: parseLastJson and the relay tolerate leading noise, but scp does not.
- **Check:** laptop: `ssh -o BatchMode=yes wsu-grid true | wc -c`. Pass = 0.
- **Looks like:** `Received message too long 1349281121`; `protocol error: mtime.sec not present`; `status did not return JSON`; `installed scripts did not answer`
- **Repair:** The file belongs to the student, so nothing is written automatically. The resolver can show the noisy lines and propose wrapping them in `if [[ $- == *i* ]]; then ... fi`, applied only on confirmation.
- **By hand:** Move echo, module load and conda activate output inside an interactive-only guard.
- **Could break when:** C&IT adds a banner to /etc/bashrc, or a module system that prints on load.
- **Code:** extension/src/core/gridApi.ts:224-237; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:504-508; extension/src/core/relay.ts:42-60

### `grid-login.bashrc-noninteractive-order` Block is sourced for non-interactive ssh (no earlier return guard)

Depends on: `grid-login.bashrc-block`, `account.grid-login-shell`

- **Assumes:** Red Hat bash (built with SSH_SOURCE_BASHRC) sources ~/.bashrc for non-interactive ssh on the login node and on compute nodes. No return/exit/exec guard may precede the Ashley block, and cmd_install APPENDS the block at the end, so any earlier guard hides it from Remote-SSH's install script.
- **Check:** laptop: `ssh -o BatchMode=yes wsu-grid 'echo "$VSCODE_SERVER_CUSTOM_GLIBC_LINKER|$PATH"'` shows a non-empty linker and ~/.ashley/bin in PATH. Repeat on the worker node: `ssh <node>.nas.grid.wayne.edu 'echo $VSCODE_SERVER_CUSTOM_GLIBC_LINKER'` (needs a running job there). Static check on the login node: `awk '/^# >>> ashley/{exit} /^[^#]*(return|exit|exec )/{print NR": "$0}' ~/.bashrc` is empty.
- **Looks like:** `exit code 207`; `found GLIBC 2.17`; `The remote host may not meet VS Code Server's prerequisites for glibc and libstdc++`; `ashley: command not found`
- **Repair:** Rewrite the block at the TOP of ~/.bashrc, before any guard. This is safe because the block only exports variables.
- **By hand:** Move the block above any `[ -z "$PS1" ] && return` line.
- **Could break when:** A C&IT /etc/bashrc that exits early for non-interactive shells, a distro whose bash lacks SSH_SOURCE_BASHRC, or Remote-SSH starting a login shell that reads ~/.bash_profile instead.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:117-124; docs/manual/90_troubleshooting.md:22; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:255

### `grid-login.home-path-layout` Grid home is /wsu/home/<2>/<4>/<AccessID>

Depends on: `account.wsu-access-id`

- **Assumes:** The laptop computes the grid home path without asking the grid. It uses that path as the default folder for Remote-SSH and as ASHLEY_GRID_HOME.
- **Check:** laptop: `ssh -o BatchMode=yes wsu-grid 'echo $HOME'` equals gridHome(accessId).
- **Looks like:** `Remote-SSH: folder does not exist`; `ASHLEY_GRID_HOME points to a missing path`
- **Repair:** Replace the computed path with the $HOME the grid reports, and cache it in settings.
- **By hand:** Type the real home path in the folder prompt.
- **Could break when:** A home-filesystem migration (for example, Panasas retirement) changes the layout.
- **Code:** extension/src/core/services.ts:96-106; extension/src/extension.ts:259

### `grid-login.home-storage` Home filesystem has room; quota readable

Depends on: `grid-login.sshd-pubkey-batchmode`

- **Assumes:** The Panasas home under its soft quota holds ~/.ashley: secrets, account dirs (CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR and transcripts), inboxes, outboxes, events and logs. `pan_quota` prints used and soft GB on the first numeric line after two header lines. If it fails, the code falls back to df.
- **Check:** login node: `pan_quota | awk 'NR>2 && $1 ~ /^[0-9.]+$/ {print $1,$2; exit}'` prints two numbers with used/soft < 0.9, and `touch ~/.ashley/.probe && rm ~/.ashley/.probe` succeeds.
- **Looks like:** `Disk quota exceeded`; `home quota 0/0 GB or ?/? GB in doctor`; `zero-byte secrets.env, state.json or outbox result`
- **Repair:** Never deletes automatically. Can offer to prune ~/.ashley/workers/*/outbox/*.events.jsonl older than 30 days, after confirmation.
- **By hand:** Move large files to /rs/rs_grp_oschome/<id>.
- **Could break when:** If Panasas is retired, pan_quota disappears. df on the home mount then reports the whole filesystem instead of the user quota, so the doctor shows a wrong number and no error.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:158-162, 210, 221, 239

### `grid-login.shared-home-coherence` ~/.ashley coherent between login and compute nodes

Depends on: `grid-login.home-storage`

- **Assumes:** A file written under ~/.ashley on the login node becomes visible on the worker's node within the 250 ms poll. rename is atomic for .part->.json, .json->.claimed and outbox writes. state.json mtimes are comparable with the login node clock: status marks a worker alive when mtime is within 90 s, and peerNote uses last_beat.
- **Check:** login node with a live worker: `echo $(( $(date +%s) - $(stat -c %Y ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/state.json) ))` < 20. Clock check: `srun --jobid=<job> --overlap -N1 -n1 -t 1 date +%s` differs from the login `date +%s` by < 2.
- **Looks like:** `worker job RUNNING but alive=false`; `queued <id> on worker <w> (job N RUNNING; k waiting) and never answered`; `a request processed twice`
- **Repair:** The filesystem cannot be repaired from here. When only clock skew is at fault, judge liveness by last_beat inside state.json instead of the file mtime.
- **By hand:** Report skew or stale caching to hpc@wayne.edu with the node name.
- **Could break when:** Moving home to NFS with attribute caching would delay inbox visibility by seconds and make mtime liveness flap. Four readdirs per second per worker on the home filesystem could draw a C&IT complaint.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:165-174, 310-317; grid/ashleyd.js:32-34, 116-117, 134-136, 398-419

### `grid-login.ashley-cli-runs` ~/.ashley/bin/ashley exists, is executable and answers

Depends on: `grid-login.sshd-pubkey-batchmode`, `grid-login.home-storage`

- **Assumes:** ~/.ashley/bin/ashley exists, has LF line endings, is executable (home not mounted noexec) and answers `version` with no environment loaded.
- **Check:** laptop: `ssh -o BatchMode=yes wsu-grid '~/.ashley/bin/ashley version'` exits 0 and prints an integer.
- **Looks like:** `No such file or directory (exit 127, shown as 'Ashley is not installed on the grid yet')`; `Permission denied (exit 126)`; `/bin/bash^M: bad interpreter`; `the grid part is not installed yet`
- **Repair:** Rerun wizard step gridInstall: mkdir, scp the six files, then `ashley install`, which strips CR and chmods.
- **By hand:** Run 'Ashley: Install or update Ashley on the grid'.
- **Could break when:** Home remounted noexec.
- **Code:** extension/src/core/wizard.ts:465-469, 527-543; extension/src/core/gridApi.ts:47-51; grid/ashley:108-111

### `grid-login.ashley-scripts-complete` All grid scripts present, same release, LF endings

Depends on: `grid-login.ashley-cli-runs`, `grid-login.sftp-subsystem`

- **Assumes:** ~/.ashley/bin holds ashley, ashleyd.js, bootstrap.sbatch, worker.sbatch.tpl, bashrc.block and VERSION from one release, all with LF endings. access_request.sbatch is created on demand.
- **Check:** login node: `cd ~/.ashley/bin && ls ashley ashleyd.js bootstrap.sbatch worker.sbatch.tpl bashrc.block VERSION && ! grep -l $'\r' ashley *.sbatch *.tpl *.js *.block`. Stronger: compare `sha256sum` of each file with the extension's dist/grid copies.
- **Looks like:** `sbatch: error: Batch script contains DOS line breaks (\r\n)`; `sed: can't read /wsu/home/.../.ashley/bin/worker.sbatch.tpl: No such file or directory`; `Error: Cannot find module '.../.ashley/bin/ashleyd.js' in slurm_<job>.out`
- **Repair:** Re-upload the bundled files, then run `ashley install`.
- **By hand:** Run 'Ashley: Install or update Ashley on the grid'.
- **Could break when:** A new release adds a file (for example phone.js, which ashleyd.js already tries to require) that gridScriptFiles does not ship.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:108-111, 277-280, 556, 597; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:458-461; grid/ashleyd.js:100-106

### `grid-login.version-match` Grid scripts version equals the extension's bundled version

Depends on: `grid-login.ashley-cli-runs`

- **Assumes:** The grid's VERSION equals extension/dist/grid/VERSION. The gridInstall check reports 'todo' on a mismatch, and the relay's READY line carries the version.
- **Check:** laptop: compare `ssh wsu-grid '~/.ashley/bin/ashley version'` with the extension's dist/grid/VERSION.
- **Looks like:** `the grid has Ashley v1; this extension brings v2`; `ashley: unknown command 'X' (ashley help)`
- **Repair:** Rerun wizard step gridInstall. Running workers keep the old ashleyd.js until they restart, so offer `worker stop` then `start`.
- **By hand:** Run 'Ashley: Install or update Ashley on the grid'.
- **Could break when:** VS Code auto-updates the extension ahead of the grid scripts, and long-running workers keep executing the previous daemon.
- **Code:** extension/src/core/wizard.ts:492-510; grid/VERSION; grid/ashley:25, 395

### `grid-login.gnu-userland` bash >= 4.2 and GNU coreutils on the login node

Depends on: `account.grid-login-shell`

- **Assumes:** The CLI needs no python, jq or node on the login node. It needs bash >= 4.2 (associative arrays and `read -t 0.25` in relay), GNU coreutils (`date +%s%3N`, `stat -c`, `sed -i`, `sleep 0.5`, `head -c`, `tail -c +N`, `xargs -r`), GNU grep -o, awk getline, getent, hostname and printf %q.
- **Check:** login node: `bash -c 'declare -A a=([x]=1); read -t 0.01 v </dev/null; date +%s%3N; stat -c %Y ~; sleep 0.01; printf %q "a b"; echo ok'`. Pass = a 13-digit millisecond time and 'ok' at the end.
- **Looks like:** `declare: -A: invalid option`; `read: 0.25: invalid timeout specification`; `date prints a literal 3N`; `stat: illegal option -- c`
- **By hand:** Report the host; Ashley requires a GNU userland.
- **Could break when:** Low for EL8/9 (bash 4.4/5.1). Only a non-GNU userland would break it.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:21, 28, 110, 329-343, 393-397, 421-422

### `grid-login.status-json-contract` status/library/access --json emit one parseable JSON line

Depends on: `grid-login.ashley-cli-runs`, `grid-slurm.squeue-format`, `lab-folder.library-json`

- **Assumes:** `ashley status --json` prints one JSON object as its last stdout line, within the extension's 90 s timeout. Fields: version, ready, awaiting_access, root, runtime, claude_version, container, source, library, bootstrap_job/state, home_*_gb, workers[], accounts[], secrets[], jobs[]. Worker state.json and library.json are embedded raw, so one malformed file breaks the whole object. The same holds for library, worker list, access whoami/status/requirements and admin list with --json.
- **Check:** laptop: `ssh wsu-grid '~/.ashley/bin/ashley status --json' | tail -1 | node -e "JSON.parse(require('fs').readFileSync(0,'utf8'))"` exits 0. Parse on the laptop because python3 and jq may be absent on the EL7 login node.
- **Looks like:** `status did not return JSON`; `installed scripts did not answer`; `Cannot reach the grid`; `tree stuck on 'Grid installing'`
- **Repair:** If a worker's state.json fails JSON.parse, move it aside; the daemon rewrites it every 10 s. If library.json is invalid, flag it for the admin.
- **By hand:** Run `ashley status --json` on the grid and read which embedded file is broken.
- **Could break when:** A new raw-embedded field, or Slurm names/reasons containing control characters.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:165-174, 202-217, 70; extension/src/core/gridApi.ts:47-53, 224-237; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:504-525

### `grid-login.config-env` config.env complete, sourceable, 600

Depends on: `grid-login.home-storage`

- **Assumes:** ~/.ashley/config.env (mode 600) is a bash-sourceable KEY=%q file. It holds ROOT, RUNTIME, SANDBOX, CLAUDE_BIN, BINDS, CONTAINER, SOURCE, BOOTSTRAP_JOB/STATE and, optionally, AWAITING_ACCESS. set_cfg writes it through one shared `$CFG.part` name, so two concurrent writers can race (the tree's `status`, which auto-adopts, and the MCP server). bootstrap.sbatch also writes it.
- **Check:** login node: `bash -n ~/.ashley/config.env && (. ~/.ashley/config.env; for v in ROOT RUNTIME SANDBOX CLAUDE_BIN BINDS; do [ -n "${!v}" ] || echo unset $v; done; [[ $BOOTSTRAP_JOB =~ ^[0-9]*$ ]] || echo bad-job)` prints nothing, and `stat -c %a ~/.ashley/config.env` = 600.
- **Looks like:** `REFUSING: no config.env`; `REFUSING: RUNTIME unset in config.env (bootstrap not finished?)`; `REFUSING: /wsu/home/.../.ashley/config.env missing; run 'ashley install' first`; `config.env must set ROOT`
- **Repair:** If the library is readable, `ashley adopt <default>` rewrites RUNTIME, SANDBOX, CLAUDE_BIN and BINDS. Otherwise `ashley install` restores ROOT. Lab admin only: `ashley bootstrap --resubmit`.
- **By hand:** Run the wizard's grid step again.
- **Could break when:** A later version adds keys that older adopt paths do not write.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:24, 30, 38-41, 94, 100; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:27-30, 123-130; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:26, 32

### `grid-login.ready-state` READY: container and Claude Code resolvable from the login node

Depends on: `grid-login.config-env`, `lab-folder.shared-container-readable`

- **Assumes:** ready means READY is non-empty, CLAUDE_BIN is executable and $SANDBOX/usr/local/bin/node is executable, all seen from the login node (CephFS is mounted there). `status` and `worker start` auto-adopt the library default once it becomes readable.
- **Check:** login node: `ashley status --json` shows "ready":true. Or directly: `test -s ~/.ashley/READY && . ~/.ashley/config.env && test -x "$CLAUDE_BIN" && test -x "$SANDBOX/usr/local/bin/node"`.
- **Looks like:** `grid not ready yet (bootstrap job N: FAILED); try again when 'ashley status' says ready=true`; `no container yet: it comes with lab access (the last setup step; your lead approves it)`; `FAIL not ready (bootstrap job none: )`
- **Repair:** If the library is readable, run `ashley adopt`. If the user is the lab admin, run `ashley bootstrap --resubmit --partition secp --qos secondary`. A student without access has no repair; they wait for access.
- **By hand:** Finish the lab-access step; the lead approves it.
- **Could break when:** The recorded absolute paths go stale (see grid-node.runtime-path).
- **Code:** grid/ashley:31, 205, 235, 258-260

### `grid-login.root-writable` Storage ROOT exists and is writable

Depends on: `lab-folder.volume-mounted`, `grid-login.config-env`

- **Assumes:** ROOT exists and is writable; workspaces live there. The default is /rs/rs_grp_oschome/$USER/ashley when that directory exists and is writable, else ~/ashley.
- **Check:** login node: `. ~/.ashley/config.env; test -d "$ROOT" -a -w "$ROOT"`.
- **Looks like:** `ashley: cannot create ROOT=...`; `FAIL ROOT not writable (unset)`; `REFUSING: cannot write <ROOT>`
- **Repair:** `ashley install` without --root falls back to default_root, sets it and runs mkdir.
- **By hand:** Set ashley.gridRoot to a writable path and rerun the grid step.
- **Could break when:** The CephFS path is renamed at a storage migration, or the group allocation expires.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:42-44, 113-116, 234; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:41

### `grid-login.secrets-env` secrets.env present, 600, with the laptop's token names

Depends on: `laptop.secret-storage`, `grid-login.sshd-pubkey-batchmode`, `grid-login.home-storage`

- **Assumes:** ~/.ashley/secrets.env (mode 600) holds GITHUB_TOKEN, GH_TOKEN and HF_TOKEN as KEY=value lines. The laptop writes it over ssh stdin under umask 077, never as an argument. It is bash-sourceable, and the daemon reads it into every run's environment.
- **Check:** login node: `stat -c %a ~/.ashley/secrets.env` = 600, and the secrets[] names in `ashley status --json` include every token the laptop holds.
- **Looks like:** `FAIL secrets.env missing (GitHub / Hugging Face tokens for the worker)`; `grid is missing: GitHub token`; `no secrets.env`
- **Repair:** pushTokens from the laptop's SecretStorage (part of wizard step gridInstall).
- **By hand:** Run 'Ashley: Install or update Ashley on the grid'.
- **Could break when:** A token format with characters that bash `source` interprets.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:189-194, 238, 634-638; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:472-480, 511-518

### `grid-login.claude-account` An enabled Claude credential under ~/.ashley/accounts

Depends on: `grid-login.home-storage`, `laptop.secret-storage`

- **Assumes:** ~/.ashley/accounts/default/token (600) holds a `claude setup-token` subscription token, or accounts/apikey/apikey holds an API key. The account is not disabled, and any state.json cooldown_until is in the past.
- **Check:** login node: `ashley accounts list` shows at least one entry with "enabled":true and cooldown_until null or past.
- **Looks like:** `FAIL no Claude account (setup wizard step 'Claude token', or ashley accounts add-token default)`; `no enabled Claude account`; `status: rate_limited`
- **Repair:** Re-push the token from the laptop. Run `ashley accounts enable <name>` only when cooldown_until has passed; that clears the stale state.json.
- **By hand:** Run 'Ashley: Store or replace my Claude token for the grid'.
- **Could break when:** The token expires one year after `claude setup-token`.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:175-188, 237, 370-381; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:481-488

### `grid-login.outbound-https` Login node reaches HF, GitHub and Anthropic APIs on 443

Depends on: `grid-login.sshd-pubkey-batchmode`

- **Assumes:** verify-tokens and access requirements run curl from the login node straight to huggingface.co, api.github.com and api.anthropic.com, with no proxy and a 15 s limit.
- **Check:** login node: `for u in https://huggingface.co https://api.github.com https://api.anthropic.com; do curl -s -m 10 -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code} $u\n" $u; done`. Pass = no 000.
- **Looks like:** `HF_TOKEN rejected or unreachable`; `GITHUB_TOKEN rejected or unreachable`; `CLAUDE_TOKEN present (the check answered HTTP 000)`; `the Claude token could not be confirmed right now`
- **Repair:** Run the same verification inside a worker allocation (compute nodes have internet), for example with `srun --overlap` using the container's curl.
- **By hand:** Report the egress block to hpc@wayne.edu.
- **Could break when:** C&IT restricts login-node egress, a common hardening step. The HF and GitHub checks do not tell 'unreachable' from 'rejected', so a blocked network looks like a bad token.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:500-507, 634-659

### `grid-login.curl-tls-ca` EL7 curl: -K - config on stdin, TLS 1.2, CA bundle still trusted

Depends on: `grid-login.outbound-https`

- **Assumes:** CentOS 7 curl 7.29 (NSS) reads `-K -` config from stdin with `header = "..."` lines, which keeps tokens out of ps, and supports `-w '%{http_code}'`. It must still negotiate TLS 1.2 with these APIs. Its CA bundle has been frozen since CentOS 7 went EOL (June 2024) and must still trust their roots.
- **Check:** login node: `printf 'header = "X-Probe: 1"\n' | curl -s -m 10 -K - -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' https://api.github.com/` = 200, and `curl -sS -m 10 -o /dev/null https://api.anthropic.com/; echo $?` is not 35 or 60.
- **Looks like:** `curl: (60) Peer's Certificate issuer is not recognized.`; `curl: (35) SSL connect error`; `NSS error -12286`; `HTTP code 000`
- **Repair:** Retry with `--cacert "$SANDBOX/etc/ssl/certs/ca-certificates.crt"` (the Debian 12 bundle on CephFS is readable from the login node).
- **By hand:** Ask C&IT to update ca-certificates, or run verification inside a worker.
- **Could break when:** High. Root CA rotations or TLS 1.3-only endpoints break an unpatched EL7 curl. A login-node OS upgrade fixes this.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:499-507

### `grid-login.verify-hf-token` HF_TOKEN verified by whoami-v2

Depends on: `grid-login.secrets-env`, `grid-login.curl-tls-ca`, `external.huggingface-api`

- **Assumes:** GET https://huggingface.co/api/whoami-v2 with Authorization: Bearer returns JSON with a "name". The first "name" match is taken as the user, but it could be an organisation's name.
- **Check:** login node: `ashley verify-tokens | grep '^HF_TOKEN'` prints 'HF_TOKEN ok (<name>)'.
- **Looks like:** `HF_TOKEN rejected or unreachable`; `HF_TOKEN missing`; `Hugging Face token missing or rejected`
- **Repair:** If the laptop's stored copy verifies from the laptop, re-push it (pushTokens).
- **By hand:** Create a new read token at huggingface.co/settings/tokens and store it with the wizard.
- **Could break when:** whoami-v2 renamed or versioned, or the JSON shape changes.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:639-642, 578

### `grid-login.verify-github-token` GITHUB_TOKEN verified by /user

Depends on: `grid-login.secrets-env`, `grid-login.curl-tls-ca`, `external.github-api`

- **Assumes:** GET https://api.github.com/user with Bearer returns a "login".
- **Check:** login node: `ashley verify-tokens | grep '^GITHUB_TOKEN'` prints 'GITHUB_TOKEN ok (<login>)'.
- **Looks like:** `GITHUB_TOKEN rejected or unreachable`; `GITHUB_TOKEN missing`; `GitHub token missing or rejected`
- **Repair:** Re-push from the laptop if the laptop's copy verifies. Otherwise re-run VS Code's GitHub sign-in (wizard step github).
- **By hand:** Sign in to GitHub again in the wizard.
- **Could break when:** Fine-grained PAT expiry (at most one year, shorter under org policy), or GitHub API version-header deprecation.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:643-646, 579

### `grid-login.verify-claude-token` Claude subscription token accepted by GET /v1/models

Depends on: `grid-login.claude-account`, `grid-login.curl-tls-ca`, `external.anthropic-api`

- **Assumes:** GET https://api.anthropic.com/v1/models with Bearer <oauth token>, `anthropic-beta: oauth-2025-04-20` and `anthropic-version: 2023-06-01` answers 200 for a valid subscription token and 401 for an invalid one. `access requirements` passes only on 'ok'. Any other code blocks the lab-access request.
- **Check:** login node: `ashley verify-tokens | grep '^CLAUDE_TOKEN'` prints 'CLAUDE_TOKEN ok (subscription token accepted)'.
- **Looks like:** `CLAUDE_TOKEN rejected (invalid, expired or revoked: run claude setup-token again)`; `CLAUDE_TOKEN present (the check answered HTTP 403)`; `CLAUDE_TOKEN present (the check answered HTTP 000)`; `the Claude token on the grid was rejected (expired or revoked)`; `CLAUDE_TOKEN missing`
- **Repair:** On 000/429/5xx, retry after 60 s. As a fallback proof, run a one-turn `claude -p 'reply ok' --max-turns 1` inside a worker. There is no repair for a 401.
- **By hand:** Run `claude setup-token` on the laptop and store the new token.
- **Could break when:** High. The OAuth beta header is undocumented for this use. If Anthropic retires `oauth-2025-04-20` or refuses OAuth tokens on /v1/models, every valid token fails this check and every lab-access request is blocked. Tokens also expire after one year.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:647-658, 573-577

### `grid-login.relay-session` Long-lived `ashley relay` tolerated on the login node

Depends on: `grid-login.ashley-cli-runs`, `grid-login.gnu-userland`, `grid-login.bashrc-quiet`

- **Assumes:** Each laptop process (extension, MCP server, CLI) keeps one `ashley relay` bash loop on the login node, sleeping in 250 ms reads, for hours. C&IT's login-node process policy must tolerate it. The relay prints `READY <ms> <version>` first and answers PING with PONG.
- **Check:** laptop: `printf 'PING\nQUIT\n' | ssh -o BatchMode=yes wsu-grid '~/.ashley/bin/ashley relay'` prints 'READY <13 digits> <n>' and then 'PONG <13 digits>' within 5 s.
- **Looks like:** `relay send failed (...); using a fresh ssh`; `relay ask failed (...); using a fresh ssh`; `Connection to warrior.grid.wayne.edu closed by remote host.`; `Killed`; `ERROR unknown command`
- **Repair:** Built in: GridRelay reconnects, and every call falls back to a fresh ssh at about 2 s.
- **By hand:** None needed unless the fallback also fails.
- **Could break when:** C&IT deploys a login-node reaper (Arbiter-style cgroup limits or a maximum session age) that kills idle long sessions. The fallback path keeps working, only slower.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:383-428; extension/src/core/relay.ts:36-60; extension/src/core/gridApi.ts:110-137

### `grid-login.ssh-connection-rate` sshd tolerates Ashley's burst of parallel connections

Depends on: `grid-login.sshd-pubkey-batchmode`

- **Assumes:** sshd MaxStartups/MaxSessions, and any C&IT rate limiting, tolerate the extension's bursts of parallel BatchMode connections. A tree refresh alone opens status, library, access whoami and access list; doctor and relay reconnects add more. Whether fail2ban or similar is deployed is unknown.
- **Check:** laptop: start 6 `ssh -o BatchMode=yes wsu-grid true` in parallel. Pass = all exit 0 within 30 s.
- **Looks like:** `kex_exchange_identification: read: Connection reset by peer`; `ssh_exchange_identification: Connection closed by remote host`; `ssh: connect to host warrior.grid.wayne.edu port 22: Connection timed out (after repeated failures)`
- **Repair:** Serialise grid calls and back off exponentially (capped at 60 s). Stop retrying after a publickey failure so that a bad key cannot trigger a ban.
- **By hand:** Wait 10 to 15 minutes. If the laptop's IP was banned, contact hpc@wayne.edu.
- **Could break when:** C&IT tightens MaxStartups or adds fail2ban.
- **Code:** extension/src/vscode/tree.ts:58-117; extension/src/core/shell.ts:103-108

### `grid-login.uninstall-clean` ashley uninstall leaves a fresh account

Depends on: `grid-login.ashley-cli-runs`

- **Assumes:** `ashley uninstall [--purge-root]` cancels worker jobs, removes the bashrc block and deletes ~/.ashley. It does NOT cancel a held ashley_access job and does not touch the admin-side status file or ACLs, so a reinstalled account can reappear as filed, pending or approved.
- **Check:** login node, after uninstall: `test ! -e ~/.ashley && ! grep -q '# >>> ashley' ~/.bashrc && [ -z "$(squeue -h -u $USER -n ashley_access -o %i)" ]`.
- **Looks like:** `reinstalled account shows 'filed' or 'pending' from before`; `ashley_* jobs left in squeue`
- **Repair:** `scancel` any of the user's jobs named ashley_* left after uninstall.
- **By hand:** Ask the lead for `ashley access admin reset <id>` if a clean request is wanted.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:660-669

### `grid.login-shell-bash` Grid login shell is bash

Depends on: `network.grid-login`

- **Assumes:** The remote side of every ssh call is parsed by a POSIX/bash login shell. That covers the single-quoted argv, `~/'...'` tilde expansion, `bash -c`, `$USER` in squeue, and the relay path left unquoted.
- **Check:** grid: `getent passwd $USER | cut -d: -f7` ends in /bash.
- **Looks like:** `Unmatched '.`; `~/.ashley/bin/ashley: No such file or directory with a tcsh shell`
- **By hand:** Ask hpc@wayne.edu to set the login shell to /bin/bash.
- **Could break when:** Account provisioning defaults changing.
- **Code:** extension/src/core/shell.ts:84-113; extension/src/core/relay.ts:40; extension/src/core/gridApi.ts:56

### `grid.shell-startup-silent` Non-interactive login prints nothing

Depends on: `network.grid-login`

- **Assumes:** ~/.bashrc and /etc/bashrc write nothing to stdout for non-interactive ssh. SFTP-mode scp breaks on stray output, and JSON answers are parsed from the last line.
- **Check:** grid: `ssh wsu-grid true` produces 0 bytes on stdout.
- **Looks like:** `Received message too long 1349281121`; `scp: Connection closed`; `status did not return JSON`
- **By hand:** Guard echo lines in ~/.bashrc with `[[ $- == *i* ]]`, or redirect them to stderr.
- **Could break when:** C&IT adding a login banner to /etc/bashrc, or a module load that prints to stdout.
- **Code:** extension/src/core/gridApi.ts:240-253; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:534

### `grid.bashrc-effective-noninteractive` Sysroot variables reach non-interactive ssh

Depends on: `grid.bashrc-block`, `grid.login-shell-bash`

- **Assumes:** RHEL bash sources ~/.bashrc for ssh commands (SSH_SOURCE_BASHRC), and the Ashley block comes before any return guard. The existing node only greps that the block exists.
- **Check:** grid: `ssh wsu-grid printenv VSCODE_SERVER_CUSTOM_GLIBC_LINKER` prints the path from the block.
- **Looks like:** `The remote host may not meet VS Code Server's prerequisites for glibc`; `exit code 207`; `printenv prints nothing`
- **Repair:** Move the managed block to the top of ~/.bashrc (awk rewrite, idempotent).
- **By hand:** Put the block above `[ -z "$PS1" ] && return` or `case $- in *i*)` in ~/.bashrc.
- **Could break when:** A new /etc/skel/.bashrc with an early return. An OS whose bash lacks SSH_SOURCE_BASHRC.
- **Code:** grid/bashrc.block:1-9; grid/ashley:117-124

### `grid.gnu-userland` bash 4 and GNU tools on the login node

Depends on: `grid.scripts`

- **Assumes:** The grid CLI is bash and coreutils only. It needs bash >= 4.2 (local -A, read -t 0.25), GNU date %3N, stat -c, sed -i, sort -V, find -perm /mode, timeout, tail -c +N, awk getline, getent and curl -K -.
- **Check:** grid: `bash -c 'declare -A a; read -t 0.01 </dev/null; date +%s%3N; stat -c %Y ~; sort -V </dev/null; timeout 1 true; find ~ -maxdepth 0 -perm /o=w'` exits 0 with 13-digit ms.
- **Looks like:** `declare: -A: invalid option`; `read: 0.25: invalid timeout specification`; `date: invalid format`
- **By hand:** Contact hpc@wayne.edu.
- **Could break when:** A login-node image change (unlikely to drop GNU, but a minimal image might lack getfacl or timeout).
- **Code:** grid/ashley:21-29,417-421,696-697

### `grid.home-path-layout` Grid home path matches gridHome()

Depends on: `network.grid-login`

- **Assumes:** $HOME = /wsu/home/<ab>/<ab12>/<accessid>. The extension builds the Remote-SSH folder and ASHLEY_GRID_HOME from this formula.
- **Check:** grid: `ssh wsu-grid 'echo $HOME'` equals gridHome(accessId).
- **Looks like:** `Remote-SSH: folder does not exist`; `ASHLEY_GRID_HOME points to a missing path`
- **Repair:** Store the reported $HOME and use it instead of the formula.
- **By hand:** Open the folder that `echo $HOME` prints.
- **Could break when:** A C&IT home migration (a new storage system or path scheme).
- **Code:** extension/src/core/services.ts:96-107; extension/src/extension.ts:243

### `grid.quota-tool` Home quota readable

Depends on: `grid.scripts`

- **Assumes:** `pan_quota` (Panasas) exists and its output has the used and soft GB in columns 1-2 after line 2. The df fallback reports filesystem totals, not the user's quota.
- **Check:** grid: `command -v pan_quota`, and the awk parse yields two numbers. Warn when home_quota fell back to df.
- **Looks like:** `home quota ?/? GB`; `home at 3 % (df on a multi-PB filesystem)`
- **By hand:** Update the quota parser for the new tool.
- **Could break when:** Panasas replaced (VAST, Ceph), pan_quota output changing.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:158-162,735-741

### `grid.home-shared-coherent` Home coherent across nodes

Depends on: `grid.scripts`, `node.probe`

- **Assumes:** The file queue needs the login node and the compute nodes to see the same $HOME, with an atomic rename(2) and mkdir, and attribute caching short enough that inbox, outbox and state.json changes show up within ~1 s and heartbeats within 90 s.
- **Check:** grid (slow): inside the worker allocation (srun --overlap), write a nonce file with .part+rename. On the login node, time until `cat` sees it. Pass under 5 s. Also mkdir the same lock dir twice (the second must fail with EEXIST).
- **Looks like:** `worker alive=false although the daemon logs beats`; `result never appears on the login node`; `two phone hosts at once`
- **By hand:** Report to hpc@wayne.edu (home filesystem caching).
- **Could break when:** A home filesystem change with longer attribute caching, or non-atomic rename across nodes.
- **Code:** extension/src/core/protocol.ts:1-14; grid/ashleyd.js:131-139,426-448; grid/phone.js:205-241

### `grid.clock-sync` Login and compute node clocks agree

Depends on: `node.probe`

- **Assumes:** Several things compare timestamps from different nodes. Heartbeat age is the file mtime compared with the login node's `date` (90 s). Cooldowns, the phone lease (60 s stale) and relay skew correction depend on the same agreement.
- **Check:** grid: inside the worker `date +%s%3N` vs the login node's, one srun round trip apart. Pass when |skew| < 5 s.
- **Looks like:** `daemon stopped answering N s ago while the log shows beats`; `phone lease taken over repeatedly`
- **By hand:** Report NTP drift to hpc@wayne.edu.
- **Could break when:** NTP misconfiguration after a node reimage.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:169-171,784-789; grid/phone.js:191-241

### `grid.secret-file-modes` Grid secrets private

Depends on: `grid.scripts`

- **Assumes:** ~/.ashley and accounts/ are 700. secrets.env, accounts/*/token and apikey are 600. No ACL entry or default ACL (for example on Panasas or a group-shared home) grants others read.
- **Check:** grid: `stat -c %a` on each, and `getfacl -p` shows no user: or group: entries with r. Pass = modes as listed.
- **Looks like:** `secrets.env present (644)`; `group::r-- on accounts/`
- **Repair:** chmod 700 ~/.ashley ~/.ashley/accounts ~/.ashley/remote; chmod 600 ~/.ashley/secrets.env ~/.ashley/accounts/*/{token,apikey}; setfacl -b on them.
- **By hand:** Run the repair, then renew tokens if they were exposed.
- **Could break when:** A home restore or migration resetting modes.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:40,108,395-398; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:479-487

### `grid.root-writable` Ashley ROOT writable

Depends on: `grid.scripts`

- **Assumes:** ROOT from config.env (/rs/rs_grp_oschome/<id>/ashley or ~/ashley) exists and is writable. Session working directories and workspaces/phone live there. `ashley doctor` checks this, `ashley diag` does not.
- **Check:** grid: `. ~/.ashley/config.env; [ -w "$ROOT/workspaces" ]`.
- **Looks like:** `cannot create ROOT=`; `EACCES: permission denied, mkdir '<ROOT>/workspaces/<session>'`
- **Repair:** `mkdir -p $ROOT/workspaces`. If the volume is gone, reset ROOT to ~/ashley via set_cfg.
- **By hand:** Ask the lead about the lab volume, or set ashley.gridRoot.
- **Could break when:** CephFS allocation moved or expired.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:42-44,113-116,234; grid/ashleyd.js:36,411

### `grid.authorized-keys-strictmodes` sshd StrictModes satisfied

Depends on: `network.grid-login`

- **Assumes:** The home directory is not group- or world-writable, ~/.ssh is 700 and authorized_keys is 600. Otherwise sshd ignores the key.
- **Check:** grid: `stat -c %a ~ ~/.ssh ~/.ssh/authorized_keys`. Pass = home without g+w/o+w, 700, 600.
- **Looks like:** `Authentication refused: bad ownership or modes for directory`; `Permission denied (publickey,keyboard-interactive) after it worked`
- **Repair:** chmod go-w ~; chmod 700 ~/.ssh; chmod 600 ~/.ssh/authorized_keys (only while still logged in).
- **By hand:** Log in with the password and code once and run those chmods.
- **Could break when:** A group-sharing change to the home directory (setfacl or chmod g+w by a tool).
- **Code:** extension/src/core/sshInstall.ts:27-28; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:349

### `grid.identity-lookup` User and group lookups (sssd)

Depends on: `grid.scripts`

- **Assumes:** getent passwd and getent group answer through sssd. admin_ingest silently drops applicants it cannot resolve, and approve refuses them.
- **Check:** grid: `getent passwd $USER` and `getent group oschome` return lines within 5 s.
- **Looks like:** `no grid user <id>`; `requests silently not ingested`
- **By hand:** Retry later. Report an sssd outage to hpc@wayne.edu.
- **Could break when:** Directory migration (AD or IPA changes).
- **Code:** grid/ashley:490,547-555,636

### `grid.module-system` Environment modules and the pinned modules

Depends on: `grid.scripts`

- **Assumes:** /etc/profile.d/modules.sh defines `module` in batch and `bash -lc` shells, and apptainer/1.2.2 (pinned in bootstrap) and singularity/3.5.2 exist. discover_runtime loads the default apptainer.
- **Check:** grid: `bash -lc 'module -t avail apptainer 2>&1'` lists apptainer/1.2.2. Warn when only a different version exists.
- **Looks like:** `module: command not found`; `Lmod has detected the following error: The following module(s) are unknown: "apptainer/1.2.2"`; `STOP: no container runtime found`
- **Repair:** Pass the newest listed apptainer version to bootstrap and repair the configured RUNTIME (grid.runtime).
- **By hand:** Lead republishes the container with the current module.
- **Could break when:** Module version retired or renamed, Lmod migration, el7 to el8 module trees.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:47-57; grid/ashley:707

### `grid.process-reaper` Long relay survives on the login node

Depends on: `grid.relay`

- **Assumes:** The long-lived `ashley relay` ssh session (read -t 0.25 loop) is not killed by a login-node reaper or an idle timeout.
- **Check:** laptop: count 'relay closed' events in the Ashley log over 24 h. Warn when above 12, or when the relay dies at a regular age.
- **Looks like:** `relay closed (ssh exit 255)`; `relay closed (ssh exit 137)`
- **Repair:** Already reconnects. Lengthen ServerAliveInterval or reconnect proactively before the observed age.
- **By hand:** Nothing needed (the fresh-ssh fallback works). Tell hpc@wayne.edu if it is killed as abuse.
- **Could break when:** C&IT deploying Arbiter-style reapers or a TMOUT for non-interactive sessions.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:407-452; extension/src/core/relay.ts:35-71

### `grid.session-transcripts-present` Named sessions still have transcripts

Depends on: `grid.accounts`, `container.claude-transcript-layout`

- **Assumes:** Each ~/.ashley/sessions/<name>.json session_id has a transcript under ~/.ashley/projects/*/<id>.jsonl. Claude Code deletes transcripts after cleanupPeriodDays (30). resolveSession still passes --resume, which then fails every time.
- **Check:** grid: for each sessions/*.json, find ~/.ashley/projects -name '<sid>.jsonl'. Pass = all found.
- **Looks like:** `No conversation found with session ID:`; `claude exited 1 without a result`
- **Repair:** Drop session_id from the stale sessions/<name>.json (keep cwd) so the next turn starts fresh. Set cleanupPeriodDays high in each account's settings.json.
- **By hand:** Start the session again under a new name.
- **Could break when:** Claude Code changing the retention default or the transcript path.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:399-423

### `grid.vscode-server-disk` Old VS Code servers pruned

Depends on: `grid.home-quota`

- **Assumes:** Remote-SSH installs one ~200 MB server per VS Code release under ~/.vscode-server on the quota'd Panasas home and never removes the old ones.
- **Check:** grid: `du -sm ~/.vscode-server/cli/servers/*`. Warn when there are more than 3 versions or more than 1 GB.
- **Looks like:** `Disk quota exceeded during server install`; `Failed to install the VS Code Server`
- **Repair:** Remove server versions other than the newest two that are not in use (no process with that commit).
- **By hand:** Remote-SSH: Uninstall VS Code Server from Host.
- **Could break when:** Monthly VS Code releases.
- **Code:** extension/src/extension.ts:252-265

## Slurm (45)

### `grid-slurm.worker-job` A worker job is RUNNING (as seen from the laptop)

Depends on: `grid-login.ashley-cli`

- **Assumes:** At least one Ashley worker job is RUNNING on a single compute node and reports its node name.
- **Check:** Laptop: `ssh -o BatchMode=yes wsu-grid '~/.ashley/bin/ashley worker list --json'` has an entry with job_state RUNNING and a non-empty node.
- **Looks like:** `No Claude worker is running on the grid.`; `job_state PENDING (Priority)`
- **Repair:** After confirmation, resubmit with the last spec (commandWorkerStart).
- **By hand:** Run 'Ashley: Start a Claude worker on the grid'.
- **Could break when:** Partition or QoS changes. Wall-time policy.
- **Code:** extension/src/extension.ts:223-230,285-330; extension/src/core/gridApi.ts:104-113

### `grid-slurm.pam-slurm-adopt` ssh to a node works only while holding a job there

Depends on: `network.proxyjump-to-nodes`, `grid-slurm.worker-running`

- **Assumes:** A compute node's sshd admits a user only while that user has a running job on the node (pam_slurm_adopt), and the session joins that job's cgroup with its CPU, memory and pids limits. Ashley connects only to the worker's node or to a node that squeue lists as RUNNING.
- **Check:** On the login node, `squeue -u $USER -h -t R -o %N` returns a node. From the laptop, `ssh -o BatchMode=yes -o ConnectTimeout=120 <node>.nas.grid.wayne.edu true` exits 0. This is slow, 23 to 101 s (see network.node-sshd-latency). Skip when no job is running.
- **Looks like:** `Access denied by pam_slurm_adopt: you have no active jobs on this node`; `Connection closed by UNKNOWN port 65535`; `Authentication failed.`
- **Repair:** Offer to start a worker (commandWorkerStart), then wait for RUNNING and a node file. This spends the student's allocation, so ask first.
- **By hand:** Start a worker, wait until the Ashley view shows its node, then use Open my grid files.
- **Could break when:** ssh to nodes disabled in favour of `srun --pty` only; changes in how sessions are adopted for multi-node or requeued jobs.
- **Code:** extension/src/extension.ts:232-283; extension/src/core/sshConfig.ts:23-25; docs/manual/90_troubleshooting.md; docs/RESEARCH.md:102

### `grid-slurm.access-request-visibility` Lab-access request as a held Slurm job the admin can see

Depends on: `network.grid-login`, `grid-login.ashley-cli-installed`

- **Assumes:** A request is `sbatch --parsable --hold -J ashley_access -p reqp -q requeue -t 1 -n 1 --mem=16M -o /dev/null --comment 'ashley-access v1 note=...'`. The admin's `squeue -h -n ashley_access -t PD -o '%i|%u|%k|%V'` must see every user's pending jobs and their comments, so PrivateData must not include jobs. The site must not purge held jobs before the admin reads them.
- **Check:** On the login node: `scontrol show config | grep -i PrivateData` shows no 'jobs' in the value. The grid diag grid.slurm-visibility tests for '= none', which also flags harmless values such as 'usage'. After a student files a request, `squeue -h -u $USER -n ashley_access -t PD -o %i` returns an id, and the admin sees the same job with its comment in the unfiltered query.
- **Looks like:** `could not file the request: sbatch: error: ...`; `Invalid qos specification`; `Invalid partition name specified`; `the admin's list stays empty while the student sees 'filed'`; `the state returns to 'none' (the held job was purged)`
- **Repair:** On the student's side, file again only when the state is 'none' and no request job exists (one per user). Safe.
- **By hand:** The admin approves directly with 'Grant a user directly', and asks C&IT about PrivateData and purges of held jobs.
- **Could break when:** A Slurm upgrade enabling PrivateData=jobs; the reqp partition or the requeue QoS being renamed; comments trimmed; held jobs reaped automatically.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:441-453,465,527-538,593-607,705-706

### `grid-slurm.client-commands` Slurm client commands on PATH for non-interactive ssh

Depends on: `grid-login.sshd-pubkey-batchmode`

- **Assumes:** sbatch, squeue, sacct, scancel and scontrol are on PATH in a non-interactive ssh session on the login node, with no `module load`.
- **Check:** login node: `command -v sbatch squeue sacct scancel scontrol` prints five paths; `sbatch --version` prints 'slurm X.Y.Z'.
- **Looks like:** `sbatch: command not found`; `ashley: sbatch failed: ...: command not found`; `status shows no jobs and bootstrap_state empty (job_state silently returns empty)`
- **Repair:** If a slurm module exists, source /etc/profile.d/modules.sh and `module load slurm`, then re-run.
- **By hand:** Report the host to hpc@wayne.edu.
- **Could break when:** An OS upgrade moves Slurm into a module or /opt. A major Slurm upgrade changes option syntax.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:32-37, 80, 152, 281, 288, 599; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:11-13

### `grid-slurm.controller-reachable` slurmctld answers; errors are not read as 'job gone'

Depends on: `grid-slurm.client-commands`

- **Assumes:** slurmctld answers within a few seconds. job_state() discards stderr, so an unreachable controller and a finished job both return an empty string. During an outage, status therefore reports no jobs, and `worker start` or `bootstrap` may submit duplicates.
- **Check:** login node: `scontrol ping` prints 'Slurmctld(primary) at ... is UP', and `timeout 15 squeue -h -u $USER -o %i >/dev/null; echo $?` = 0.
- **Looks like:** `slurm_load_jobs error: Socket timed out on send/recv operation`; `slurm_load_jobs error: Unable to contact slurm controller (connect failure)`; `ashley status exceeds the extension's 90 s timeout`
- **Repair:** Retry with backoff. The resolver must NOT treat an empty job_state as 'gone', and must block resubmission, while `scontrol ping` fails.
- **By hand:** Wait; check C&IT maintenance announcements.
- **Could break when:** Slurm upgrade windows and controller failover.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:32-37, 145-148, 263-264; extension/src/core/gridApi.ts:47-53

### `grid-slurm.sacct-accounting` sacct returns final states of finished jobs

Depends on: `grid-slurm.controller-reachable`

- **Assumes:** slurmdbd is up, and sacct returns the final state of jobs that have left squeue after MinJobAge. job_state keeps the first word, so 'CANCELLED by 123' becomes CANCELLED.
- **Check:** login node: `sacct -n -X -P -o State -S now-1days | head -1; echo $?` = 0. For the bootstrap: `sacct -j $BOOTSTRAP_JOB -n -X -P -o State,ExitCode`.
- **Looks like:** `sacct: error: Problem talking to the database: Connection refused`; `sacct: error: Slurm accounting storage is disabled`; `bootstrap job N: (empty state) in status and doctor`
- **Repair:** Read the job's own log instead: in ~/.ashley/log/bootstrap_<id>.out, 'READY written' means success and 'STOP:' or 'REFUSING' means failure.
- **By hand:** Report to hpc@wayne.edu if it persists.
- **Could break when:** Slurm versions change sacct formats or state names.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:36, 209, 235

### `grid-slurm.squeue-format` squeue format codes and filters behave as parsed

Depends on: `grid-slurm.controller-reachable`

- **Assumes:** squeue accepts -h, -u, -n, -t PD and -j, and the format codes %i %j %T %N %M %l %P %R (8 '|'-separated fields) plus %u, %k (comment) and %V (submit time). %N is a short node name. The laptop's GridApi.squeue() passes '$USER' through shq, so it is single-quoted and never expanded. This was found by reading gridApi.ts:56 with shell.ts:86-107 and has not been run. Consequence: the 'connect to another running job' picker and the MCP 'My Slurm jobs' resource always see an empty list.
- **Check:** login node: `squeue -h -u $USER -o '%i|%j|%T|%N|%M|%l|%P|%R' | awk -F'|' 'NF<8{b=1} END{exit b}'` and `squeue -h -n ashley_access -o '%i|%u|%k|%V'` both exit 0. laptop: `ssh wsu-grid "'squeue' '-u' '\$USER' '-h'"` reproduces the quoting bug.
- **Looks like:** `squeue: error: Invalid user: $USER`; `No Claude worker is running. Connect to another running job? never offered`; `fields shifted when a reason contains '|'`
- **Repair:** This is a code fix, not a runtime repair: GridApi.squeue should use `ashley status --json` jobs[], or `bash -c 'squeue -u "$USER" ...'`.
- **By hand:** Use `ashley status` on the grid to see jobs.
- **Could break when:** Slurm moves to --json output and deprecates text formats, or changes comment-field semantics (AccountingStoreFlags).
- **Code:** grid/ashley:195-201, 459, 531; extension/src/core/gridApi.ts:55-58; extension/src/core/shell.ts:86-108; extension/src/core/slurm.ts:16-38; extension/src/extension.ts:248, 400; extension/src/mcp/server.ts:155-156

### `grid-slurm.parsable-submit` sbatch --parsable output captured as a clean job id

Depends on: `grid-slurm.client-commands`, `account.slurm-association`

- **Assumes:** `sbatch --parsable` prints only `<jobid>[;cluster]`. All four submit sites capture `2>&1`: library publish, bootstrap, worker start and access request. Any stderr line from sbatch or a site job_submit plugin (a warning, 'info: defaulting ...') therefore becomes part of the stored id (BOOTSTRAP_JOB in config.env, workers/<w>/job.id).
- **Check:** login node: every workers/*/job.id and BOOTSTRAP_JOB matches ^[0-9]+$. One-time probe: `sbatch --parsable --hold -J ashley_probe -p secp -q secondary -t 1 --mem=16M -o /dev/null --wrap true 2>&1` is a bare integer; scancel it afterwards.
- **Looks like:** `job.id or BOOTSTRAP_JOB containing 'sbatch: warning:' text`; `worker job_state 'none' right after a successful start`; `a second worker for the same name PENDING (Dependency)`
- **Repair:** Rewrite job.id or BOOTSTRAP_JOB with the last line matching ^[0-9]+, or look the job up by name: `squeue -h -u $USER -n ashley_<w> -o %i`.
- **By hand:** Run `ashley worker stop --name <w>` and start again.
- **Could break when:** C&IT adds a job_submit.lua that prints notices on stderr.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:80-81, 152-154, 281-282, 599-600

### `grid-slurm.partition-reqp-requeue` reqp/requeue accepts and starts Ashley jobs

Depends on: `account.slurm-association`, `grid-slurm.parsable-submit`

- **Assumes:** reqp/requeue is the default for bootstrap.sbatch (4 CPU, 16G, 2 h), for workers (4 CPU, 16G, 24 h) and for the held request job, and it starts jobs in reasonable time. On 2026-09-28 it pended 30+ minutes on Priority while secp/secondary started at once.
- **Check:** login node: `sbatch --test-only -p reqp -q requeue -c 4 --mem=16G -t 1-00:00:00 --wrap true 2>&1` prints 'to be started at <time>'. Pass = within an hour; later = warn.
- **Looks like:** `squeue reason (Priority) or (Resources) for hours`; `Requested time limit is invalid (missing or exceeds some limit)`; `Invalid qos specification`
- **Repair:** If a worker or bootstrap has pended more than 15 minutes on Priority/Resources and `--test-only` predicts an earlier start on secp/secondary, scancel it and resubmit there (`worker start --partition secp --qos secondary`, or `bootstrap --resubmit --partition secp --qos secondary`).
- **By hand:** Set ashley.worker.partition=secp and ashley.worker.qos=secondary.
- **Could break when:** reqp is renamed or retired, or the requeue QoS policy changes.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:8-10, 15; grid/ashley:143, 247, 599; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:258, 299

### `grid-slurm.partition-secp-secondary` secp/secondary usable as fallback and publish default

Depends on: `account.slurm-association`

- **Assumes:** Students may use secp/secondary, and its MaxWall (90 days per sacctmgr on 2026-09-28) covers requests. Library publish defaults to it. Unknown: whether secondary is preemptible. Workers get --requeue only when qos=requeue, so a preempted secondary worker would be cancelled rather than requeued.
- **Check:** login node: `sbatch --test-only -p secp -q secondary -c 4 --mem=16G -t 1-00:00:00 --wrap true 2>&1`, then `sacctmgr -nP show qos secondary format=name,maxwall,preemptmode` and `scontrol show partition secp | grep -E 'PreemptMode|AllowQos|MaxTime'`.
- **Looks like:** `Invalid qos specification`; `Job's QOS not permitted to use this partition`; `worker state PREEMPTED or CANCELLED with no restart`
- **Repair:** If secondary preempts, add --requeue for it as well (a code change), or restart the worker when sacct shows PREEMPTED.
- **By hand:** Start the worker again.
- **Could break when:** secp is an owned-node scavenger partition and can change with hardware ownership.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:75, 143, 267-269; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:299

### `grid-slurm.partition-gpu` GPU workers: gmsap,gvohp,galhp + QoS gpu + gres gpu

Depends on: `account.slurm-association`

- **Assumes:** GPU workers are submitted with `--partition gmsap,gvohp,galhp --qos gpu --gres=gpu:N`. The account must be allowed QoS gpu on those partitions, and the gres name 'gpu' must exist. The partition list is hard-coded in the extension.
- **Check:** login node: `sinfo -h -p gmsap,gvohp,galhp -o '%P %a %G'` lists all three as up with gpu gres, and `sbatch --test-only -p gmsap,gvohp,galhp -q gpu --gres=gpu:1 -c 4 --mem=16G -t 04:00:00 --wrap true 2>&1` prints 'to be started'.
- **Looks like:** `sbatch: error: Invalid generic resource (gres) specification`; `Job's QOS not permitted to use this partition`; `sbatch: error: invalid partition specified: galhp`
- **Repair:** Remove from the list any partition that sinfo does not list or reports inactive, then retry.
- **By hand:** Ask hpc@wayne.edu which GPU partitions the account may use.
- **Could break when:** High. Lab-owned GPU partitions are renamed or retired as hardware ages.
- **Code:** extension/src/core/slurm.ts:69-71; extension/src/extension.ts:291; grid/ashley:268; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:18, 42

### `grid-slurm.qos-limits-and-pairs` Offered (partition, QoS) pairs valid; hours within MaxWall

Depends on: `account.slurm-association`

- **Assumes:** Every (partition, QoS) pair the UI offers is valid. The MCP tool accepts qos 'primary' with partition reqp, which is probably not a permitted pair (unverified). QoS MaxWall and MaxSubmitJobsPerUser must allow the requested --hours and one held request job alongside the workers.
- **Check:** login node: `sacctmgr -nP show qos requeue,secondary,primary,gpu format=name,maxwall,maxsubmitpu,maxjobspu,preemptmode` and `scontrol show partition reqp secp | grep -E 'AllowQos|MaxTime'`.
- **Looks like:** `Invalid qos specification`; `Job violates accounting/QOS policy (job submit limit, user's size and/or time limits)`; `QOSMaxSubmitJobPerUserLimit`; `pending reason QOSMaxWallDurationPerJobLimit`
- **Repair:** Clamp --hours to the QoS MaxWall. Refuse, or remap, a pair that sacctmgr/scontrol say is not allowed before submitting.
- **By hand:** Pick reqp/requeue or secp/secondary.
- **Could break when:** C&IT QoS policy changes.
- **Code:** extension/src/mcp/server.ts:60-65; grid/ashley:265-266; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:299

### `grid-slurm.requeue-preemption` requeue QoS preempts with REQUEUE; workers resume, bootstrap does not

Depends on: `grid-slurm.partition-reqp-requeue`

- **Assumes:** QoS requeue is preemptible with PreemptMode REQUEUE. Workers on it carry --requeue and --open-mode=append and resume (the daemon is re-entrant and recovers .claimed files). bootstrap.sbatch on the same QoS carries --no-requeue, so a preempted bootstrap ends PREEMPTED or CANCELLED and must be resubmitted.
- **Check:** login node: `scontrol show config | grep -E '^(PreemptMode|PreemptType|JobRequeue)'` and `sacctmgr -nP show qos requeue format=name,preemptmode`. After an event: `sacct -j <id> -o State,Restarts`.
- **Looks like:** `bootstrap job N: PREEMPTED (no READY written)`; `repeated 'ashleyd start' lines in ashleyd.log (requeued)`; `requests retried with attempt > 1`
- **Repair:** If the bootstrap state is PREEMPTED, NODE_FAIL or TIMEOUT, run `ashley bootstrap --resubmit` (on secp/secondary if reqp pends). If a worker was not requeued, run `ashley worker start`.
- **By hand:** Start the worker again; queued requests wait in the inbox.
- **Could break when:** A PreemptMode change to CANCEL makes requeue workers die at the first preemption.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:9-10; grid/ashley:267-269; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:14-19; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:139-143

### `grid-slurm.singleton-dependency` --dependency=singleton keeps one job per worker name

Depends on: `grid-slurm.partition-reqp-requeue`

- **Assumes:** --dependency=singleton, keyed on job name ashley_<name> per user, keeps one worker per name. A stale PENDING or RUNNING twin makes a new submission wait with reason Dependency.
- **Check:** login node: `squeue -h -u $USER -o '%j %i %T %R' | awk '$1 ~ /^ashley_/{c[$1]++} END{for(k in c) if(c[k]>1) print k}'` is empty (the held ashley_access job excepted).
- **Looks like:** `worker PENDING (Dependency) indefinitely`; `worker <w> already has job N (PENDING)`
- **Repair:** Keep the job whose id is in job.id and scancel the other jobs of the same name.
- **By hand:** `ashley worker stop --name <w>`, then start it again.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:6, 17; grid/ashley:262-264

### `grid-slurm.batch-signal` --signal=B:TERM@120 reaches the daemon before the wall limit

Depends on: `grid-slurm.worker-alive`

- **Assumes:** --signal=B:TERM@120 delivers TERM to the batch shell about 2 minutes (up to 3) before the wall limit. The script `exec`s apptainer, so the batch shell's PID becomes apptainer, which should forward the signal to node ashleyd.js, whose SIGTERM handler calls beginStop. Not tested on this grid.
- **Check:** compute node test job (secp/secondary, -t 00:04:00): render the worker template with a fake claude. Pass = ashleyd.log contains 'SIGTERM' and state.json shows stopping before the job ends in TIMEOUT.
- **Looks like:** `job ends TIMEOUT with no 'SIGTERM' in ashleyd.log`; `in-flight request without a result in outbox`; `<id>.json.claimed left in inbox (recovered at next start)`
- **Repair:** Restart the worker; the daemon re-queues .claimed files at start.
- **By hand:** Resend the lost request.
- **Could break when:** Slurm changes signal timing, or apptainer changes signal forwarding.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:16, 45-47; grid/ashleyd.js:112-113, 426-450

### `grid-slurm.submit-dir-output` --output paths resolve against the submit directory

Depends on: `grid-slurm.client-commands`

- **Assumes:** #SBATCH --output resolves against the submit directory. ashley cds into ~/.ashley/log (bootstrap, request) or the worker directory (log/slurm_%j.out) before calling sbatch. The worker's log/ must exist beforehand, or the job has nowhere to write.
- **Check:** login node, after submit: `ls ~/.ashley/log/bootstrap_<id>.out` exists; once RUNNING, `ls ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/log/slurm_<id>.out` exists.
- **Looks like:** `worker log shows nothing under '--- slurm'`; `job FAILED at once with no output file`
- **Repair:** `mkdir -p` the log directories and resubmit.
- **By hand:** Run `ashley worker start --name <w>` again (it creates log/).
- **Code:** grid/ashley:152, 261, 281, 296, 599; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:16; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:14

### `grid-slurm.export-environment` --export semantics: ALL carries PATH; commas split values

Depends on: `grid-slurm.client-commands`

- **Assumes:** sbatch's default --export=ALL carries the ssh session's environment into jobs: PATH with ~/.ashley/bin from the bashrc block, HOME, USER. `--export=ALL,K=V,...` splits on commas, so a value containing a comma is cut. `library publish --purpose 'general: Claude Code, Node 22, git, python3'`, exactly as INSTALL.md shows, stores 'general: Claude Code'. Found by reading, not run.
- **Check:** login node, after a publish: `grep -o '"purpose": *"[^"]*"' /rs/rs_grp_oschome/shared/ashley/library.json` equals the requested text.
- **Looks like:** `library.json purpose truncated at the first comma`; `stray variables such as ' Node 22' in the publish job environment`
- **Repair:** Pass PURPOSE through a file, or base64-encode it, instead of --export (a code fix).
- **By hand:** Use a purpose without commas.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:76, 80, 150-152; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:102-112; docs/INSTALL.md:89-90

### `grid-slurm.held-request-job` Lab-access request = held zero-work Slurm job

Depends on: `account.slurm-association`, `grid-slurm.parsable-submit`, `lab-folder.access-requirements`

- **Assumes:** A request is `sbatch --hold -J ashley_access -p reqp -q requeue -t 1 -n 1 --mem=16M -o /dev/null --comment 'ashley-access v1 note=...'` of a two-line script. It stays PENDING (JobHeldUser), uses no storage, and is cancelled by tidy_request_job after the decision. Slurm authenticates the submitter, which stops forgery. Unknown: whether C&IT purges long-held jobs, or counts them against a per-user submit limit.
- **Check:** login node (student): `squeue -h -u $USER -n ashley_access -o '%i|%T|%r|%k'` prints one line: PENDING|JobHeldUser|ashley-access v1 note=....
- **Looks like:** `ashley: could not file the request: sbatch: error:`; `state flips from 'filed' back to 'none' (held job vanished before ingest)`; `your request is already with your lead (filed), indefinitely`
- **Repair:** If the state is none, no status file exists and the requirements pass, refile with the stored note. If reqp/requeue refuses, file on secp/secondary (the job never runs).
- **By hand:** Run the wizard's last step again, or tell the lead directly.
- **Could break when:** A purge policy for held jobs, a job_submit plugin that rejects --mem=16M or -t 1, or Slurm hiding --comment from squeue.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:459, 508-520, 587-600

### `grid-slurm.squeue-privatedata` squeue shows every user's jobs (PrivateData lacks jobs)

Depends on: `grid-slurm.squeue-format`

- **Assumes:** squeue shows every user's jobs, so the admin's ingest `squeue -h -n ashley_access -t PD -o '%i|%u|%k|%V'` sees students' held requests. The code comments state this, but it has not been exercised with a second account.
- **Check:** login node (admin): `scontrol show config | grep -i '^PrivateData'` shows none, or a list without 'jobs'; `squeue -h -t PD -o %u | sort -u | wc -l` > 1.
- **Looks like:** `admin queue empty while the student's access status says filed`; `tree shows 0 pending`
- **By hand:** If C&IT enables PrivateData=jobs, ask for an exception or move requests to a drop-box design.
- **Could break when:** High. PrivateData=jobs is a common hardening step and would silently stop every access request.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:435-441, 521-532

### `grid-slurm.srun-overlap` srun --jobid --overlap can probe inside a worker allocation

Depends on: `grid-slurm.worker-alive`

- **Assumes:** `srun --jobid=<id> --overlap` (Slurm >= 20.11) starts a step inside a running worker allocation. The resolver uses it to run compute-node checks without submitting a job; the 2026-09-28 peer test used it too.
- **Check:** login node: `srun --jobid=$(cat ~/.ashley/workers/default/job.id) --overlap -N1 -n1 -t 1 hostname` prints the worker node within 30 s.
- **Looks like:** `srun: error: Unable to create step for job N: Requested nodes are busy`; `srun: unrecognized option '--overlap'`
- **Repair:** Fall back to a tiny probe job: `sbatch --wait -p secp -q secondary -t 2 --mem=100M --wrap '<check>'`.
- **By hand:** 
- **Could break when:** Slurm changes step-sharing defaults.
- **Code:** docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:110-114; docs/research/library_peer_test.sh

### `grid-slurm.worker-template-render` worker.sbatch.tpl rendered with no leftover tokens

Depends on: `grid-login.ashley-scripts-complete`, `grid-slurm.batch-scripts-lf`

- **Assumes:** `ashley worker start` renders worker.sbatch.tpl with sed using '|' as the delimiter. The values (partition, QoS, mem, sandbox and claude paths, library root) must contain no '|', '&' or newline ('&' in a sed replacement inserts the match), and no @TOKEN@ may remain.
- **Check:** Login node: grep -c '@[A-Z_]*@' ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/job.sbatch -> 0; grep -E '^#SBATCH --(partition|qos|time|mem|cpus-per-task)=' job.sbatch shows concrete values.
- **Looks like:** `sbatch: error: invalid partition specified: @PARTITION@`; `sed: -e expression #1, char N: unknown option to s'`; `a sandbox path mangled where '&' was replaced by the match`; `sed: -e expression #N, char M: unknown option to s'`; `REFUSING: sandbox @SANDBOX@ incomplete`
- **Repair:** Re-render with ashley worker start --name <w> using validated values (hours integer, mem like 16G).
- **By hand:** Avoid special characters in worker settings; reinstall grid scripts if the template itself is damaged.
- **Could break when:** New placeholders added to the template in a later Ashley version without the matching sed expression in an older grid/ashley.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:6-19,28,42,47; grid/ashley:265-281

### `grid-slurm.worker-alive` Worker job RUNNING with a fresh heartbeat

Depends on: `grid-slurm.partition-reqp-requeue`, `grid-login.ready-state`, `grid-node.runtime-path`, `grid-node.dev-shm`, `grid-login.shared-home-coherence`, `container.sandbox-exec`, `container.claude-code-binary`

- **Assumes:** A worker is healthy when its job is RUNNING, workers/<w>/node has been written and state.json is younger than 90 s. The job script refuses early, with REFUSING lines, when config, sandbox, claude or runtime is missing on the node.
- **Check:** laptop or login node: `ashley worker list --json` shows job_state RUNNING and alive true. Otherwise run `ashley worker log --name <w>` and grep slurm_<job>.out for REFUSING.
- **Looks like:** `REFUSING: no config.env`; `REFUSING: RUNTIME unset in config.env (bootstrap not finished?)`; `REFUSING: sandbox <path> incomplete`; `REFUSING: <claude> missing`; `REFUSING: runtime <path> missing on <host>`; `REFUSING: no /dev/shm scratch`; `job_state RUNNING with alive false`
- **Repair:** Stop and start the worker once. If a REFUSING line names config or sandbox, run `ashley adopt` first.
- **By hand:** Send the output of 'Ashley: Show worker log' to the lead.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:26-47; grid/ashley:165-174, 256-302

### `grid-slurm.slurm-in-worker-container` Slurm and the ashley CLI reachable from inside a worker

Depends on: `grid-slurm.worker-alive`

- **Assumes:** peerNote tells the worker Claude that '~/.ashley/bin/ashley is on PATH' and that it can run `ashley worker start`. Inside the apptainer container, PATH is the container's own plus only the claude directory, so ~/.ashley/bin is probably absent. sbatch, squeue, the munge socket and the Slurm libraries are not bound. So `ashley send`/`result` by full path should work (they only touch files), but `ashley worker start` and job_state from inside a worker should fail. Found by reading; the 2026-09-28 peer test ran on the host through `srun --overlap`, not inside the container.
- **Check:** compute node: `srun --jobid=<job> --overlap -N1 -n1 -t 1 $RUNTIME exec $BINDS $SANDBOX bash -c 'command -v ashley sbatch squeue; ls ~/.ashley/bin/ashley'`. Pass = sbatch and squeue resolve.
- **Looks like:** `bash: ashley: command not found`; `ashley: sbatch failed: ... sbatch: command not found`; `queued <id> on worker <w> (job none not running) from inside a worker`
- **By hand:** Code fix: prepend ~/.ashley/bin via APPTAINERENV_PREPEND_PATH, and either bind the Slurm clients, munge socket and slurm.conf, or tell the worker Claude that it cannot submit jobs.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:187-196, 299-316; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:45-47; grid/ashley:32-37

### `grid-slurm.bootstrap-job` bootstrap.sbatch completes (own container or library publish)

Depends on: `grid-slurm.partition-reqp-requeue`, `grid-login.config-env`, `container.apptainer-module`, `container.sandbox-build`, `external.docker-hub`, `external.npm-registry`, `grid-slurm.partitions-qos`, `grid-slurm.output-paths`, `grid-slurm.batch-scripts-lf`, `grid-node.root-storage`, `grid-login.ashley-install`

- **Assumes:** One job (5-15 min, 4 CPU, 16G, 2 h limit, --no-requeue) finds a runtime, builds or reuses the sandbox, installs the pinned Claude Code, smoke-tests, and writes READY + config.env (own) or merges library.json (LIBRARY=1, admin). Exit codes: 1 config/ROOT refused, 2 no runtime, 3 no runtime could exec, 4 npm install failed. It is idempotent: finished pieces are skipped.
- **Check:** Login node: sacct -j $BOOTSTRAP_JOB -X -n -o State,ExitCode -> COMPLETED 0:0; tail -3 ~/.ashley/log/bootstrap_$BOOTSTRAP_JOB.out contains 'READY written' (own) or 'published <name>' (library).
- **Looks like:** `STOP: no container runtime found on <host>`; `STOP: sandbox built but no runtime could exec it`; `STOP: npm install failed`; `REFUSING: cannot write <ROOT>`; `REFUSING: <cfg> missing; run 'ashley install' first`; `State TIMEOUT after 2 h`; `REFUSING: ~/.ashley/config.env missing; run 'ashley install' first`; `STOP: <npm>/bin/claude missing after install`; `LIBRARY=1 needs ROOT_OVERRIDE`
- **Repair:** `ashley bootstrap --resubmit` (on secp/secondary if reqp pends). Use `--update` to reinstall Claude Code. Exit 2 or 3: re-resolve the runtime (see grid-node.runtime-path).
- **By hand:** Read ~/.ashley/log/bootstrap_<job>.out; the STOP line names the failed step (runtime, build, npm).
- **Could break when:** Every vendor update in the chain (module, Docker Hub, npm, Claude Code) surfaces here first; the 2 h limit assumes Docker Hub and npm are fast.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:1-43,57,81,90,94,99-133; grid/ashley:72-81,139-156

### `grid-slurm.node-probe` Cheap way to run a check ON a compute node (srun --overlap into the worker, or a 1-CPU probe job)

Depends on: `network.grid-login`, `grid-login.ashley-scripts-installed`

- **Assumes:** Every compute-node check in this graph runs as a Slurm step, never as work on the login node. With a worker running: srun --jobid=$JID --overlap -N1 -n1 --quiet -t 1 <cmd> (Slurm >= 20.11 semantics of --overlap; used successfully in docs/research/library_peer_test.sh). With no worker: a probe job 'sbatch --parsable -p secp -q secondary -t 5 -c 1 --mem=1G -D ~/.ashley/log -o probe_%j.out --wrap <cmd>' starts within minutes. Throughout this graph PROBE(cmd) means one of these two, and CEXEC(cmd) means PROBE('source ~/.ashley/config.env; mkdir -p /dev/shm/${USER}_probe; $RUNTIME exec $BINDS -B /dev/shm/${USER}_probe:/tmp $SANDBOX <cmd>; rm -rf /dev/shm/${USER}_probe').
- **Check:** Login node (only waits): JID=$(cat ~/.ashley/workers/default/job.id); srun --jobid=$JID --overlap -N1 -n1 --quiet -t 1 hostname -f -> prints the same host as ~/.ashley/workers/default/node within ~10 s. No worker running: sbatch --parsable -p secp -q secondary -t 5 -c 1 --mem=1G -D ~/.ashley/log -o probe_%j.out --wrap 'hostname -f'; pass = probe_<id>.out contains a *.nas.grid.wayne.edu name and sacct State=COMPLETED.
- **Looks like:** `srun: unrecognized option '--overlap'`; `srun: error: Unable to create step for job <id>: Requested node configuration is not available`; `srun: Job <id> step creation temporarily disabled, retrying`; `srun: error: Invalid job id specified`; `probe job PENDING (Priority) for more than 10 minutes`
- **Repair:** If srun --overlap is refused, fall back to the sbatch --wrap probe; if the secp/secondary probe pends > 10 min, resubmit the same probe on reqp/requeue.
- **By hand:** Ask hpc@wayne.edu whether --overlap steps are allowed inside running jobs; meanwhile run node checks as tiny sbatch jobs and read ~/.ashley/log/probe_<id>.out.
- **Could break when:** A Slurm upgrade can change step-overlap defaults or disallow extra steps (OverlapStep policy); partition/QoS names used by the fallback probe are site-specific and may be renamed.
- **Code:** docs/research/library_peer_test.sh:25; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:111-113; docs/RESEARCH.md:135

### `grid-slurm.partitions-qos` Partitions and QoS the jobs name exist and the account may use them

Depends on: `network.grid-login`, `account.wsu-access-id`

- **Assumes:** Partition reqp with QoS requeue (bootstrap and worker default), secp with QoS secondary (the documented fallback, and the default for library publish), and GPU partitions gmsap,gvohp,galhp with QoS gpu exist, are up, and the user's Slurm association permits each pairing. galhp and the 'gpu' QoS name are not verified anywhere in the repository.
- **Check:** Login node (read-only, instant): sinfo -h -o '%P %a' -p reqp,secp,gmsap,gvohp,galhp -> each listed and 'up'; for each pair: sbatch --test-only -p reqp -q requeue -t 1 -c 1 --wrap true (and secp/secondary, gmsap/gpu --gres=gpu:1) -> stderr 'sbatch: Job <n> to start at ...' (nothing is submitted). Optional: sacctmgr -n -P show assoc user=$USER format=partition,qos.
