- **Looks like:** `sbatch: error: Batch job submission failed: Invalid partition name specified`; `sbatch: error: Batch job submission failed: Invalid qos specification`; `sbatch: error: Batch job submission failed: Invalid account or account/partition combination specified`; `Job's QOS not permitted to use this partition`; `ashley: sbatch failed: <message>`
- **Repair:** Pick the first pairing that --test-only accepts (reqp/requeue, then secp/secondary) and write it to ashley.worker.partition / ashley.worker.qos; for GPU, drop partitions that --test-only rejects from the comma list.
- **By hand:** Ask the lab admin or hpc@wayne.edu which partition/QoS the AccessID may use and set ashley.worker.partition and ashley.worker.qos accordingly.
- **Could break when:** reqp/secp/gmsap/gvohp are WSU-specific names; C&IT renames or retires partitions when hardware (H200/L40S) arrives or at the el7 -> el8/el9 migration; QoS names requeue/secondary/gpu may be consolidated.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:8-10; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:7-8; grid/ashley:75,247,268-269; extension/src/core/slurm.ts:59-75; extension/package.json (ashley.worker.partition, ashley.worker.qos)

### `grid-slurm.walltime-limits` Requested wall time fits the QoS MaxWall

Depends on: `grid-slurm.partitions-qos`

- **Assumes:** The worker's --time (hours from the extension, 1..720, rendered as D-HH:00:00) and the bootstrap's 02:00:00 are within the chosen QoS MaxWall (measured: primary 30 d, secondary 90 d; requeue and gpu unknown).
- **Check:** Login node: sacctmgr -n -P show qos requeue,secondary,primary,gpu format=name,maxwall; then sbatch --test-only -p <part> -q <qos> -t <rendered time> --wrap true -> accepted.
- **Looks like:** `sbatch: error: Batch job submission failed: Requested time limit is invalid (missing or exceeds some limit)`; `squeue reason QOSMaxWallDurationPerJobLimit`; `job PENDING forever with a limit reason`
- **Repair:** Clamp hours to the QoS MaxWall reported by sacctmgr and resubmit.
- **By hand:** Choose fewer hours or a QoS with a longer MaxWall (primary).
- **Could break when:** C&IT changes QoS limits without notice; the extension's 720 h ceiling is hard-coded.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:265-266; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:15; extension/src/extension.ts:296; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:299

### `grid-slurm.queue-wait` Worker and bootstrap jobs start within minutes

Depends on: `grid-slurm.partitions-qos`

- **Assumes:** A submitted worker or bootstrap job leaves PENDING quickly. Measured: reqp/requeue sat PENDING (Priority) 30+ minutes while secp/secondary started at once.
- **Check:** Login node: squeue -j $JID -h -o '%T|%r|%V' -> RUNNING, or PENDING younger than 10 minutes.
- **Looks like:** `PENDING (Priority)`; `PENDING (Resources)`; `PENDING (QOSMaxJobsPerUserLimit)`; `PENDING (AssocGrpCpuLimit)`; `Ashley view shows the worker as PENDING for long`
- **Repair:** For the user's own pending ashley_* job: scontrol update JobId=$JID Partition=secp QOS=secondary; if refused, scancel and resubmit with --partition secp --qos secondary (queued requests stay in the inbox, nothing is lost).
- **By hand:** Set ashley.worker.partition=secp and ashley.worker.qos=secondary, or wait.
- **Could break when:** Fair-share weights and partition policies change each semester; secondary may become preemptible (see grid-slurm.requeue-reentrancy).
- **Code:** grid/ashley:139-143; docs/manual/90_troubleshooting.md:16; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:258,299

### `grid-slurm.batch-scripts-lf` Grid scripts on disk have LF line endings

Depends on: `grid-login.ashley-scripts-installed`

- **Assumes:** bootstrap.sbatch, worker.sbatch.tpl (and the rendered job.sbatch), ashley and ashleyd.js have no CR characters; files scp'd from a Windows checkout are stripped by 'ashley install' (sed 's/\r$//').
- **Check:** Login node: file ~/.ashley/bin/* ~/.ashley/workers/*/job.sbatch | grep -c CRLF -> 0.
- **Looks like:** `sbatch: error: Batch script contains DOS line breaks (\r\n)`; `/bin/bash^M: bad interpreter: No such file or directory`; `$'\r': command not found`
- **Repair:** sed -i 's/\r$//' on the affected files (what ashley install does), then re-render job.sbatch with ashley worker start.
- **By hand:** Rerun 'Ashley: Install or update Ashley on the grid'.
- **Could break when:** A new upload path that bypasses 'ashley install', or a git core.autocrlf setting on a student's machine overriding .gitattributes.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:109-111; .gitattributes:1; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:529-543

### `grid-slurm.output-paths` #SBATCH --output resolves against the submit directory and that directory exists

Depends on: `grid-login.ashley-scripts-installed`

- **Assumes:** --output is relative to the submit directory: the worker is submitted with 'cd ~/.ashley/workers/<w> && sbatch job.sbatch' and writes log/slurm_%j.out (open-mode append); the bootstrap is submitted from ~/.ashley/log and writes bootstrap_%j.out. If the log directory is missing, Slurm cannot open stdout and the job fails with no log at all.
- **Check:** Login node: test -d ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/log -a -w ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/log && test -d ~/.ashley/log; after a job: sacct -j $JID -X -n -o State,ExitCode and ls ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/log/slurm_$JID.out exists.
- **Looks like:** `sacct State FAILED with no slurm_<id>.out or bootstrap_<id>.out file`; `ashley worker log shows '--- slurm' with nothing under it`
- **Repair:** mkdir -p ~/.ashley/log ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/{inbox,outbox,log} and resubmit.
- **By hand:** Do not delete log/ folders under ~/.ashley; rerun ashley worker start.
- **Could break when:** If submission is ever done with sbatch -D elsewhere, relative outputs move (CLAUDE.md: --output resolves against the submit dir).
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:14-15,22-23; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:5,16; grid/ashley:149-152,261,281

### `grid-slurm.singleton-job-names` --dependency=singleton job names do not collide

Depends on: `grid-slurm.worker-template-render`

- **Assumes:** Each worker job is named ashley_<worker> with --dependency=singleton, so only one runs per name per user. Ashley also uses the names ashley_access (a held request job that never runs) and ashley_bootstrap. A worker named 'access' would wait forever behind the held request (and the admin ingest, which reads 'squeue -n ashley_access -t PD', would take the pending worker for an access request); a worker named 'bootstrap' waits behind a bootstrap job. Worker-name validation (^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$) allows both.
- **Check:** Login node: squeue -u $USER -h -o '%j %T %r' | sort | uniq -c -> no ashley_<name> appears twice; ls ~/.ashley/workers contains neither 'access' nor 'bootstrap'.
- **Looks like:** `worker PENDING (Dependency) indefinitely`; `DependencyNeverSatisfied`; `an admin queue entry whose note is empty and whose job is a worker`
- **Repair:** If a same-name job is PENDING and is an older ashley_<w> of this user, scancel it; refuse worker names access and bootstrap (reserved).
- **By hand:** Rename the worker (ashley worker stop --name access; start it under another name).
- **Could break when:** Any new ashley_* job name (probe jobs, publish jobs) enlarges the collision set.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:6,17; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:7; grid/ashley:253,459,531,599

### `grid-slurm.job-state-query` squeue/sacct answer for the user's own jobs

Depends on: `network.grid-login`

- **Assumes:** job_state() asks squeue first and sacct second; an empty answer is read as 'job gone'. sacct needs slurmdbd and keeps records only for the retention period.
- **Check:** Login node: squeue -u $USER -h -o %i exits 0 within 10 s; sacct -n -X -j $BOOTSTRAP_JOB -o State returns a state for a job finished today.
- **Looks like:** `slurm_load_jobs error: Socket timed out on send/recv operation`; `sacct: error: Problem talking to the database: Connection refused`; `status shows bootstrap_state empty for a job that ran`; `worker_json job_state 'none' while the job runs`
- **Repair:** Retry after 30 s before acting on an empty state; never start a second worker while squeue errors (singleton is the backstop).
- **By hand:** Wait for slurmctld/slurmdbd; report persistent errors to hpc@wayne.edu.
- **Could break when:** Slurm upgrades change output formats; accounting retention purges old BOOTSTRAP_JOB ids so a stale id reads as gone (see the access review's stale BOOTSTRAP_JOB finding).
- **Code:** grid/ashley:32-37,144-148,263-264,315; docs/research/access_review_2026-09-28.json (grid/ashley:208 finding)

### `grid-slurm.stop-signal` Wall-time and cancel signals reach ashleyd.js through apptainer

Depends on: `grid-slurm.worker-template-render`, `container.exec-sandbox`

- **Assumes:** #SBATCH --signal=B:TERM@120 signals only the batch shell; because the template ends with 'exec $RUNTIME exec ...', the batch shell IS the apptainer process, which must forward SIGTERM to 'node ashleyd.js'. The daemon then stops claiming and kills running claude children after 90 s, inside the 120 s budget (Slurm may deliver the signal up to 60 s off). A plain scancel sends SIGTERM to every process and SIGKILL after KillWait, so the 90 s grace is not honoured there.
- **Check:** Login node, after a worker ended: grep -E 'stopping \(SIGTERM\)|^.* exit$' ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/log/ashleyd.log | tail -2 -> 'stopping (SIGTERM)' followed by 'exit'; scontrol show config | grep -E '^KillWait' (>= 30 s).
- **Looks like:** `ashleyd.log ends without 'stopping (SIGTERM)'`; `result status 'cancelled' with error 'claude exited null by SIGKILL without a result'`; `slurmstepd: error: *** JOB <id> ON <node> CANCELLED AT <time> DUE TO TIME LIMIT ***`; `state.json frozen at 'busy' after the job ended`
- **Repair:** None at runtime; the next daemon start recovers .claimed requests.
- **By hand:** Run apptainer in the background and 'wait' with a bash trap that forwards TERM (also makes the /dev/shm cleanup trap fire); raise the signal lead time.
- **Could break when:** Apptainer releases change signal forwarding in the starter; Slurm changes --signal timing; a switch to cgroup v2 changes how steps are killed.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:16,40,45-47; grid/ashleyd.js:104-106,412-431; docs/RESEARCH.md:231

### `grid-slurm.requeue-reentrancy` A requeued or preempted worker resumes without harm

Depends on: `grid-slurm.partitions-qos`, `grid-node.daemon-alive`

- **Assumes:** On QoS requeue the job carries --requeue and --open-mode=append; a requeued script restarts from the top on possibly another node, and recoverClaimed() moves *.json.claimed back to the inbox, so a request that was half done runs again from scratch (tool side effects repeat; events.jsonl is appended twice). On any other QoS the job is --no-requeue, so preemption (if secondary is preemptible) simply kills the worker.
- **Check:** Login node: scontrol show job $JID | grep -o 'Restarts=[0-9]*' -> Restarts=0 expected; grep -c 'recovered .*\.claimed' ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/log/ashleyd.log counts re-run requests; sacctmgr -n -P show qos requeue,secondary format=name,preempt,preemptmode.
- **Looks like:** `recovered <id>.json.claimed`; `slurmstepd: error: *** JOB <id> ON <node> CANCELLED AT <time> DUE TO PREEMPTION ***`; `two 'start <id>' lines for one id in ashleyd.log`; `node file changes mid-session (Remote-SSH window loses its host)`
- **Repair:** If the job ended by preemption without requeue: ashley worker start --name <w> (inbox survives).
- **By hand:** Keep requests idempotent; use primary QoS for long, side-effecting runs.
- **Could break when:** C&IT can change PreemptMode or JobRequeue globally; making secondary preemptible would silently kill secp workers.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:15,19; grid/ashley:267-269; grid/ashleyd.js:101,432-439; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:139-143; docs/RESEARCH.md:231

### `grid-slurm.gpu-gres` GPU workers get a GPU and apptainer --nv sees it

Depends on: `grid-slurm.partitions-qos`, `container.exec-sandbox`

- **Assumes:** With gpus>0 the job gets '#SBATCH --gres=gpu:N' on gmsap,gvohp,galhp (QoS gpu) and the daemon runs under 'apptainer exec --nv', which binds the host NVIDIA driver libraries into the container.
- **Check:** Login node: sinfo -h -p gmsap,gvohp,galhp -o '%P %G' shows gpu gres. Compute node: CEXEC with --nv inside a GPU worker allocation: nvidia-smi -L -> at least one 'GPU 0:' line.
- **Looks like:** `sbatch: error: Invalid generic resource (gres) specification`; `WARNING: Could not find any nv files on this host!`; `nvidia-smi: command not found`; `CUDA error: no CUDA-capable device is detected`
- **By hand:** Start a CPU worker, or ask C&IT which GPU partition/gres name applies to the account.
- **Could break when:** Driver upgrades, new gres type names (gpu:h200), GPU partitions renamed; library GPU images must still carry Node 22 at /usr/local/bin/node (see container.image-contents).
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:18,42,45; grid/ashley:267-268; extension/src/core/slurm.ts:69-70

Depends on: `grid.slurm`

### `grid.qos-limits` Requested wall time within QoS limits

Depends on: `grid.qos`

- **Assumes:** ashley.worker.hours (up to 720) is within the chosen QoS MaxWall (primary 30 d, secondary 90 d, requeue ?). Per-user job and TRES limits allow another worker. Otherwise the job pends forever.
- **Check:** grid: `sacctmgr -n -P show qos <q> format=name,maxwall,maxjobspu,maxtrespu`. Pass when hours <= maxwall. A pending worker's reason does not start with QOSMax.

### `grid.cli-output-contract` Slurm output formats Ashley parses

Depends on: `grid.slurm`

- **Assumes:** Ashley parses several outputs. `sbatch --parsable` prints id[;cluster]. `squeue -h -o '%i|%j|%T|%N|%M|%l|%P|%R'` and '%i|%u|%k|%V' (comment, submit time) print as formatted, with -t PD and -n filters. `sacct -j -n -X -o State -P` prints the state. `scontrol show config` prints 'PrivateData = ...'.
- **Check:** grid: run each against a known job (for example the worker's). Pass = the fields split into the expected count, and the job id is numeric.
