### `grid.access-request-job` Held access-request job accepted and kept

Depends on: `grid.partitions`, `grid.slurm-visibility`

- **Assumes:** `sbatch --hold -J ashley_access -p reqp -q requeue -t 1 -n 1 --mem=16M --comment ...` is accepted: the minimums are allowed, the student has the requeue QoS, and the comment is stored and shown by squeue %k. The held job also stays pending until the admin decides (no purge of held jobs).
- **Check:** grid: `sbatch --test-only --hold -p reqp -q requeue -t 1 -n 1 --mem=16M --comment probe --wrap true` exits 0. For a filed student, own_request_job is non-empty until a decision.
- **Looks like:** `could not file the request`; `Requested time limit is invalid`; `Memory specification can not be satisfied`; `state flips from filed to none without a decision`
- **Repair:** Resubmit the request when state is none but no decision exists (idempotent).
- **By hand:** The lead grants directly (Grant a user directly).
- **Could break when:** C&IT purging long-held jobs, raising the minimum memory, or restricting the requeue QoS.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:483,611-624

### `grid.sbatch-export-commas` Publish passes PURPOSE through --export

Depends on: `grid.slurm`

- **Assumes:** `sbatch --export=ALL,...,PURPOSE=$purpose` carries a free-text purpose. Commas separate --export entries, so the documented purpose 'general: Claude Code, Node 22, git, python3' would split.
- **Check:** grid: `sbatch --test-only --export='ALL,X=a, b' --wrap true` exits 0, and a published entry's purpose equals the one given.
- **Looks like:** `sbatch: error: Invalid environment variable name`; `library entry purpose truncated at the first comma`
- **By hand:** Pass the purpose in a file or base64-encode it (a code change). Until then, use purposes without commas.
- **Could break when:** Slurm --export parsing changes.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:74-81; docs/INSTALL.md (library publish example)

### `grid.srun-overlap` srun --overlap into the worker's job

Depends on: `grid.worker`

- **Assumes:** `srun --jobid=<running worker> --overlap -N1 -n1` starts a step inside the student's own allocation (Slurm >= 20.11). diag-node, grid_tests.sh and worker-to-worker tests use it.
- **Check:** grid: `timeout 60 srun --jobid=<jid> --overlap -N1 -n1 --quiet hostname` prints the worker's node.
- **Looks like:** `srun: error: Unable to create step for job`; `srun: unrecognized option '--overlap'`; `step creation temporarily disabled, retrying`
- **By hand:** Restart the worker. Report to hpc@wayne.edu if steps are refused.
- **Could break when:** Slurm changes to step and overlap semantics.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:804-811; tools/grid_tests.sh:23-25

### `grid.worker-directives` Worker job directives honoured

Depends on: `grid.slurm`

- **Assumes:** The worker job relies on several directives. --dependency=singleton keeps one job per worker name. --signal=B:TERM@120 gives the daemon a grace period. --open-mode=append and --requeue apply on the requeue QoS. --output=log/slurm_%j.out resolves against the submit dir, because ashley cds into the worker dir before sbatch.
- **Check:** grid: `scontrol show job <jid>` shows Dependency=singleton (while pending), Requeue=1 on the requeue QoS, and StdOut under ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/log.
- **Looks like:** `two jobs for one worker running at once`; `slurm_*.out missing`; `daemon killed without 'stopping (SIGTERM)' in ashleyd.log`
- **By hand:** Stop the duplicate worker job (ashley worker stop, then start).
- **Could break when:** Slurm policy disabling requeue or singleton.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:6-19; grid/ashley:277-281

### `grid.preemption-grace` Preemption gives grace, requests replay safely

Depends on: `grid.worker-directives`

- **Assumes:** On the requeue QoS, preemption sends SIGTERM with enough grace. On restart, recoverClaimed re-runs interrupted requests from the beginning, which is safe only if prompts are idempotent. A prompt that submitted jobs may submit them twice.
- **Check:** grid: `scontrol show config | grep -E 'PreemptMode|GraceTime'` and the requeue QoS GraceTime. Warn when the grace is under 120 s. Count 'recovered' lines in ashleyd.log.
- **Looks like:** `recovered <id>.json.claimed`; `duplicate Slurm jobs after a requeue`
- **By hand:** Use secp/secondary or primary for long tasks. Write prompts so they check before resubmitting.
- **Could break when:** Preemption policy changes.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:472-479; grid/ashley:269

### `grid.worker-pending-reason` Worker not stuck pending

Depends on: `grid.worker`

- **Assumes:** A worker on reqp/requeue starts within a reasonable time. Measured: reqp pended 30+ min on Priority while secp/secondary started at once.
- **Check:** grid: squeue %R for PENDING worker jobs. Warn when pending more than 15 min with reason Priority or Resources.
- **Looks like:** `Reason=(Priority)`; `Reason=(Resources)`
- **Repair:** Offer (with consent) to resubmit the worker on secp/secondary with the same resources.
- **By hand:** Set ashley.worker.partition=secp and qos=secondary, then start the worker again.
- **Could break when:** Cluster load. Changes to the fair-share policy.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:139-141; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:258,299

## Compute node (42)

### `grid-node.vscode-sysroot` C&IT VS Code glibc sysroot present and sufficient on compute nodes

Depends on: `grid-login.bashrc-noninteractive-order`, `grid-slurm.node-probe`

- **Assumes:** VS Code Server 1.99+ needs glibc >= 2.28 and libstdc++ >= 3.4.25 (host has 2.17); C&IT provides /wsu/apps/vscode/sysroot/x86_64-linux-gnu/x86_64-linux-gnu/sysroot/{lib,lib64} (ld-linux-x86-64.so.2) and /wsu/apps/vscode/patchelf/0.19.1/bin/patchelf (>= 0.18 required). 'ashley doctor' checks these on the login node only.
- **Check:** Compute node: PROBE(bash -c 'S=/wsu/apps/vscode/sysroot/x86_64-linux-gnu/x86_64-linux-gnu/sysroot; test -e $S/lib/ld-linux-x86-64.so.2 && $S/lib/libc.so.6 | head -1; /wsu/apps/vscode/patchelf/0.19.1/bin/patchelf --version') -> a glibc version >= 2.28 and 'patchelf 0.19.1'.
- **Looks like:** `FAIL missing /wsu/apps/vscode/sysroot/x86_64-linux-gnu/x86_64-linux-gnu/sysroot/lib/ld-linux-x86-64.so.2 (C&IT moved the sysroot?)`; `exit code 207`; `version GLIBC_2.28' not found`; `version GLIBCXX_3.4.25' not found`; `FAIL missing <path> (C&IT moved the sysroot?)`; `The remote host may not meet VS Code Server's prerequisites for glibc and libstdc++`; `found GLIBC 2.17`
- **Repair:** Probe for a relocated sysroot (`find /wsu/apps/vscode -maxdepth 6 -name ld-linux-x86-64.so.2`), regenerate bashrc.block with the paths found, then run `ashley install`. If nothing is found, there is no automatic repair.
- **By hand:** Check the C&IT KB article 'How to SSH to the Grid in Visual Studio Code' for new paths, or email hpc@wayne.edu.
- **Could break when:** High. C&IT may version the sysroot path. VS Code may raise its floor past the sysroot's glibc, or drop the VSCODE_SERVER_CUSTOM_GLIBC_* escape. An EL8/9 upgrade makes the sysroot unnecessary.
- **Code:** grid/bashrc.block:4-5; grid/ashley:233; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:255

### `grid-node.vscode-patchelf` patchelf 0.19.1 at the versioned C&IT path

Depends on: `grid-login.bashrc-noninteractive-order`

- **Assumes:** /wsu/apps/vscode/patchelf/0.19.1/bin/patchelf exists and runs on compute nodes. The version number is part of the hard-coded path.
- **Check:** compute node (srun --overlap): `/wsu/apps/vscode/patchelf/0.19.1/bin/patchelf --version` prints 'patchelf 0.19.1'.
- **Looks like:** `FAIL missing /wsu/apps/vscode/patchelf/0.19.1/bin/patchelf (C&IT moved the sysroot?)`; `Remote-SSH server fails right after download`
- **Repair:** Take the newest `ls -d /wsu/apps/vscode/patchelf/*/bin/patchelf | sort -V | tail -1`, regenerate the block and run `ashley install`.
- **By hand:** Ask C&IT for the current patchelf path.
- **Could break when:** A C&IT patchelf upgrade changes the version in the path.
- **Code:** grid/bashrc.block:6; grid/ashley:233

### `grid-node.runtime-path` Recorded absolute apptainer path exists on every compute node

Depends on: `container.apptainer-module`

- **Assumes:** The RUNTIME path recorded at build time (for example /wsu/el7/gnu7/apptainer/1.2.2/bin/apptainer, stored in library.json and config.env) exists and executes on every compute node a worker lands on. The worker never runs `module load`: it checks -x on that path and execs it.
- **Check:** compute node (srun --overlap, or a 1-minute secp/secondary job): `. ~/.ashley/config.env; test -x "$RUNTIME" && "$RUNTIME" --version` prints 'apptainer version 1.2.2'.
- **Looks like:** `REFUSING: runtime /wsu/el7/gnu7/apptainer/1.2.2/bin/apptainer missing on <host>`; `FAIL runtime missing on this host (fine on the login node if the compute nodes have it)`
- **Repair:** In a tiny job: `source /etc/profile.d/modules.sh; module load apptainer` (any version), test exec of the sandbox, then set_cfg RUNTIME (student). The admin re-publishes or patches the runtime field in library.json.
- **By hand:** The lead re-publishes the library entry after a grid OS upgrade.
- **Could break when:** High. An EL7 to EL8/9 upgrade retires the /wsu/el7 tree, and an apptainer module bump (1.2.2 to 1.3/1.4) removes the versioned path.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:47-58, 125; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:35, 45; grid/ashley:90, 100, 236; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:153-155

### `grid-node.dev-shm` /dev/shm writable scratch on compute nodes

Depends on: `grid-slurm.partition-reqp-requeue`

- **Assumes:** /dev/shm on compute nodes is writable and large enough to serve as the worker's /tmp (bound with -B $SCR:/tmp) and as bootstrap scratch. Node /tmp is swept by a cleaner, so it is not used. Files in /dev/shm count against the job's memory cgroup.
- **Check:** compute node: `d=/dev/shm/${USER}_probe; mkdir -p $d && df -h /dev/shm && rmdir $d`. Pass = writable with more than 1 GB free.
- **Looks like:** `REFUSING: no /dev/shm scratch`; `No space left on device (writes to /tmp inside Claude runs)`; `job OUT_OF_MEMORY after large /tmp use`
- **Repair:** Restart the worker with `--exclude <node>` (a template option to add).
- **By hand:** Report the node to hpc@wayne.edu.
- **Could break when:** C&IT caps /dev/shm per job.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:39-41, 45; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:42

### `grid-node.hostname-fqdn` The node name the worker records matches the laptop's ProxyJump pattern

Depends on: `grid-slurm.worker-alive`, `grid-node.worker-preflight`

- **Assumes:** The job writes 'hostname -f' (e.g. asx1.nas.grid.wayne.edu) to ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/node; the laptop uses it verbatim when it contains a dot, and only 'Host *.nas.grid.wayne.edu' carries ProxyJump. Another suffix means a direct, unreachable connection.
- **Check:** Login node: cat ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/node ends with .nas.grid.wayne.edu. Laptop: ssh -G <node> | grep -i proxyjump -> proxyjump wsu-grid.
- **Looks like:** `ssh: Could not resolve hostname asx1.localdomain`; `Remote-SSH connects without ProxyJump and times out`; `worker <w> has no node yet`; `ssh: Could not resolve hostname <node>`; `Connecting with SSH timed out (no jump host in the path)`
- **Repair:** Rewrite the node file as $(hostname -s).nas.grid.wayne.edu via PROBE, or set ashley.nodeDomain to the observed suffix and rewrite the ssh block.
- **By hand:** Tell the lab admin the node domain changed.
- **Could break when:** C&IT renaming the node domain or making hostname -f return an internal/InfiniBand name.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:36; extension/src/core/sshConfig.ts:48-58,100-109; extension/src/extension.ts:239-261

### `grid-node.pam-slurm-adopt` The node's sshd admits the user while a job runs there

Depends on: `grid-slurm.worker-alive`, `grid-node.hostname-fqdn`, `vscode.remote-ssh`, `laptop.ssh-config-block`, `network.grid-login`

- **Assumes:** pam_slurm_adopt on compute nodes admits the user only while they hold a job on that node, and adopts the session into the newest job's cgroup. Remote-SSH to the worker node therefore works while the worker runs, and its VS Code server shares the worker's --mem and --cpus. The node's sshd took about 23 s before running a command (measured).
- **Check:** laptop, with a worker RUNNING on <node>: `ssh -o BatchMode=yes <node>.nas.grid.wayne.edu true` exits 0 within 60 s. From the login node without a job: `ssh <node> true` fails with the pam_slurm_adopt message.
- **Looks like:** `Access denied by pam_slurm_adopt: you have no active jobs on this node`; `Connecting with SSH timed out`; `oom-kill in slurm_<job>.out, or state OUT_OF_MEMORY, after the VS Code server joins the cgroup`; `Connection closed by UNKNOWN port 65535`; `Permission denied (publickey,gssapi-keyex,gssapi-with-mic)`
- **Repair:** Start a worker before connecting. Keep remote.SSH.connectTimeout at 300 (the extension sets it).
- **By hand:** Start a worker (Ashley connects only to nodes where the user holds a job).
- **Could break when:** C&IT disables ssh to compute nodes in favour of OnDemand, or a slower PAM stack pushes sessions past the timeout.
- **Code:** extension/src/extension.ts:232-279; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:29-32; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:246, 253

### `grid-node.cephfs-acl-enforced` Compute nodes enforce CephFS POSIX ACLs like the login node

Depends on: `lab-folder.acl-grant`, `lab-folder.volume-mounted`

- **Assumes:** Every compute node mounts CephFS with POSIX ACL enforcement identical to the login node's. An approved student outside the group, holding only named-user ACLs, can then traverse /rs/rs_grp_oschome and read and execute the shared sandbox inside a job. Not yet tested with a non-owner account.
- **Check:** compute node, run as an approved non-member: `test -r /rs/rs_grp_oschome/shared/ashley/library.json && test -x <sandbox>/usr/local/bin/node && <runtime> exec <sandbox> true`.
- **Looks like:** `REFUSING: sandbox <path> incomplete`; `FATAL: container creation failed: mount /rs/rs_grp_oschome/shared: permission denied`; `access state approved_unreadable`
- **Repair:** If the ACL is missing, the admin runs `ashley access admin resync`. Otherwise there is no automatic repair.
- **By hand:** Ticket to hpc@wayne.edu naming the node.
- **Could break when:** CephFS client changes (kernel vs fuse, the acl mount option) at an OS upgrade.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:475-487, 97-99; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:31; docs/research/access_review_2026-09-28.json

### `grid-node.internet-egress` Compute nodes reach Anthropic, npm, Docker Hub, HF, GitHub

Depends on: `external.anthropic-api`, `external.npm-registry`, `external.docker-hub`

- **Assumes:** Compute nodes have direct outbound HTTPS. Claude runs, npm install, Docker Hub pulls in bootstrap, and hf/git inside workers all depend on it. The container shares the host network namespace.
- **Check:** compute node: `curl -s -m 10 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' https://api.anthropic.com/` is non-000. Also check the bootstrap smoke line `net=` in bootstrap_<id>.out.
- **Looks like:** `smoke: ... net=000`; `npm ERR! network`; `FATAL: While making image from oci registry: ... timeout`; `API Error: Connection error`
- **By hand:** Report to hpc@wayne.edu with the node name.
- **Could break when:** C&IT introduces an egress proxy or allowlist on compute nodes.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:95-96; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:29-32

### `grid-node.module-system` Environment modules work in a batch shell and apptainer/1.2.2 is available

Depends on: `grid-slurm.node-probe`

- **Assumes:** Compute nodes have /etc/profile.d/modules.sh defining 'module' in a non-interactive batch shell, and 'module load apptainer/1.2.2' yields /wsu/el7/gnu7/apptainer/1.2.2/bin/apptainer. Other candidates tried in order: conda singularity under ~/mambaforge or ~/miniforge3, module singularity/3.5.2, /usr/bin/apptainer, /usr/bin/singularity; ~/.local/bin/singularity is deliberately skipped (partial install).
- **Check:** Compute node: PROBE(bash -c 'source /etc/profile.d/modules.sh && module -t avail apptainer 2>&1') -> lists apptainer/1.2.2.
- **Looks like:** `module: command not found`; `ERROR: Unable to locate a modulefile for 'apptainer/1.2.2'`; `ModuleCmd_Load.c(213):ERROR:105: Unable to locate a modulefile for 'apptainer/1.2.2'`; `runtime candidates: (empty)`; `STOP: no container runtime found on <host>`
- **Repair:** Pick the newest apptainer/* module that 'module -t avail' lists (sort -V | tail -1), pass it to the bootstrap and resubmit.
- **By hand:** Ask hpc@wayne.edu which apptainer module replaced 1.2.2.
- **Could break when:** The el7 -> el8/el9 migration renames module trees (/wsu/el9/...), Lmod may replace Tcl modules, and 1.2.2 will be retired; the version is hard-coded.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:45-58; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:133-137

### `grid-node.user-namespaces` Unprivileged user namespaces are enabled on compute nodes

Depends on: `container.runtime-path`

- **Assumes:** The apptainer module runs non-setuid, so both 'build --sandbox' and 'exec' need /proc/sys/user/max_user_namespaces > 0 on the node. Never use --fakeroot: uid 0 inside makes Claude Code refuse --dangerously-skip-permissions.
- **Check:** Compute node: PROBE(cat /proc/sys/user/max_user_namespaces) -> a number > 0, and CEXEC(id -u) -> the user's uid (not 0).
- **Looks like:** `Failed to create user namespace`; `user namespace not supported`; `--dangerously-skip-permissions cannot be used with root/sudo privileges for security reasons`
- **Repair:** Resubmit the job with --exclude=<node> if only some nodes have it disabled.
- **By hand:** Report the node to hpc@wayne.edu.
- **Could break when:** Security hardening after a user-namespace CVE (as some distributions did) would disable unprivileged Apptainer grid-wide.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:64-80; docs/RESEARCH.md:36,130

### `grid-node.root-storage` ROOT (container, npm prefix, caches, workspaces) is writable with room

Depends on: `grid-slurm.node-probe`

- **Assumes:** ROOT defaults to /rs/rs_grp_oschome/$USER/ashley when that directory exists and is writable, else $HOME/ashley. It holds containers/ (1.1 GB sandbox), npm/, npm-cache/, cache/ (APPTAINER_CACHEDIR), tmp/ (APPTAINER_TMPDIR, TMPDIR during the build) and workspaces/. It must be writable from compute nodes and under quota.
- **Check:** Login node: source ~/.ashley/config.env; test -w "$ROOT"; df-ceph oschome (usage < 90 %). Compute node: PROBE(bash -c 'source ~/.ashley/config.env; touch $ROOT/tmp/.p && rm $ROOT/tmp/.p').
- **Looks like:** `REFUSING: cannot write <ROOT>`; `FAIL ROOT not writable (<path>)`; `EDQUOT: disk quota exceeded`; `No space left on device`
- **Repair:** Clear $ROOT/cache and $ROOT/tmp (safe, regenerated) in a job; recreate missing subdirectories with mkdir -p.
- **By hand:** Free space on the lab volume or set ashley.gridRoot to a location with room.
- **Could break when:** The CephFS allocation (from 2026-09-21) can be resized, moved or renamed; students without /rs/rs_grp_oschome/$USER fall back to the nearly full Panasas home.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:42-44,113-116,234; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:36-43

### `grid-node.egress` Compute nodes reach the internet directly

Depends on: `grid-slurm.node-probe`

- **Assumes:** Compute nodes have outbound HTTPS without a proxy (verified to zenodo.org and api.anthropic.com), so Docker Hub, npm, Anthropic, GitHub, Hugging Face and the VS Code download CDN are reachable from jobs. Containers share the host network namespace.
- **Check:** Compute node: PROBE(curl -s -m 8 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' https://api.anthropic.com/) -> any code other than 000.
- **Looks like:** `net=000 in the bootstrap smoke line`; `curl: (7) Failed to connect`; `curl: (28) Connection timed out`
- **By hand:** Report egress loss to hpc@wayne.edu; if C&IT introduces a proxy, set HTTPS_PROXY for jobs.
- **Could break when:** A campus egress firewall, a mandatory proxy, IPv6-only changes or TLS inspection would break every external dependency at once.
- **Code:** docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:31-32; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:95-96; docs/LIMITS.md:53

### `grid-node.dev-shm-scratch` /dev/shm scratch exists, has room, and is cleaned up

Depends on: `grid-slurm.node-probe`

- **Assumes:** /tmp on nodes is swept by a cleaner, so each job uses /dev/shm/${USER}_ashley_<name>_<job> bound to /tmp inside the container (TMPDIR=/tmp; Claude's temp tree and tool output land there). tmpfs pages count against the job's memory limit. The worker template sets 'trap rm -rf $SCR EXIT' and then 'exec's apptainer, which replaces the shell, so the trap never runs and every worker job leaves its directory behind on the node.
- **Check:** Compute node: PROBE(bash -c 'df -h /dev/shm | tail -1; ls -d /dev/shm/${USER}_ashley_* 2>/dev/null') -> free space > 2 GB and every listed suffix is a job id in 'squeue -u $USER -h -o %i'.
- **Looks like:** `REFUSING: no /dev/shm scratch`; `ENOSPC: no space left on device, write`; `slurmstepd: error: Detected 1 oom-kill event(s) in StepId=<id>.batch`
- **Repair:** PROBE on that node: remove /dev/shm/${USER}_ashley_*_<jobid> and ${USER}_ashley_boot_<jobid> whose job id is not in the user's squeue list.
- **By hand:** Change the template to run apptainer in the background and 'wait', so the trap fires.
- **Could break when:** A job_container/tmpfs plugin or an epilog that cleans /dev/shm (helpful) or a smaller /dev/shm on new nodes.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:39-41,45; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:42; CLAUDE.md (Grid: /tmp is node-local and swept)

### `grid-node.kernel-cpu` The node's kernel and CPU run the Claude Code binary

Depends on: `container.claude-installed`

- **Assumes:** The host kernel (reported 5.15) and each node's CPU instruction set can run the Bun ELF; a SIGILL was reported on a CentOS 7.9 host (cause, kernel or glibc, not settled). Old nodes may lack instructions a new build selects.
- **Check:** Compute node: PROBE(bash -c 'uname -r; grep -m1 -o -w avx2 /proc/cpuinfo') and CEXEC($CLAUDE_BIN --version; echo rc=$?) -> rc=0 (not 132/135).
- **Looks like:** `Illegal instruction (core dumped)`; `exit 132`; `panic(main thread): Illegal instruction`; `Bus error (exit 135)`
- **Repair:** Record the failing node and restart the worker with --exclude=<node>.
- **By hand:** Pin the last Claude Code version that ran, or use a constraint for newer CPUs.
- **Could break when:** New Claude/Bun builds raising the ISA baseline; an OS upgrade to a RHEL 8 kernel (4.18).
- **Code:** docs/RESEARCH.md:98,108-110

### `grid-node.accounts-available` At least one enabled Claude credential directory exists

Depends on: `grid-login.ashley-scripts-installed`

- **Assumes:** ~/.ashley/accounts/<name>/ holds 'token' (setup-token, sk-ant-oat...), or 'apikey', or '.credentials.json'; files 600, directory 700; not 'disabled'; subscriptions sort before API keys, 'default' first. Token files are re-read on every request.
- **Check:** Login node: ashley accounts list -> non-empty with enabled true; stat -c %a ~/.ashley/accounts/default/token -> 600; head -c 10 ~/.ashley/accounts/default/token -> sk-ant-oat (prefix only, never the value).
- **Looks like:** `no enabled Claude account in ~/.ashley/accounts (run the Ashley setup wizard or ashley accounts add-token)`; `every enabled Claude account is on cooldown until <time>`; `worker status 'blocked'`
- **Repair:** If the laptop's SecretStorage holds a Claude token and the grid has none, push it (pushTokens: ssh stdin, umask 077); clear a cooldown only when cooldown_until is already past (ashley accounts enable <name>).
- **By hand:** Ashley: Store or replace my Claude token for the grid (runs claude setup-token).
- **Could break when:** Token format changes (the pattern expects sk-ant-oat).
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:8-20,152-165,311-357; grid/ashley:175-188,370-381; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:472-490

### `grid-node.rate-limit-detection` Limit messages are recognised and the reset time parsed

Depends on: `container.claude-stream-json-contract`

- **Assumes:** Seven regexes (hit your limit, usage limit reached, rate limit, too many requests, 429, out of usage, overloaded_error) detect exhaustion; parseResetTime handles 'try again in N' and 'resets 3:45pm' in the container's local time zone (apptainer binds /etc/localtime); 'resets Mon 12:00am' does not match and falls back to 60 minutes; stream-json's rate_limit_event (resetsAt) is ignored. The patterns exist twice (protocol.ts and ashleyd.js) and must agree.
- **Check:** Laptop: npm run test:unit (protocol.test.ts, daemon.test.ts); diff the pattern lists in protocol.ts:111-119 and ashleyd.js:57. Compute node: CEXEC(date +%Z) -> EDT/EST, not UTC.
- **Looks like:** `status error (not rate_limited) whose result text contains 'hit your'`; `hourly retries during a weekly limit`; `cooldown_until off by 4-5 hours`
- **By hand:** Parse rate_limit_event.resetsAt; keep one pattern list.
- **Could break when:** Anthropic rewords limit messages or adds weekday/timezone formats; overloaded_error (a server-side 529) is counted as the student's limit and puts the account on cooldown for an hour.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:57-81,229-250; extension/src/core/protocol.ts:109-149; extension/test-fixtures/fake-claude.js:26-29; docs/RESEARCH.md:72

### `grid-node.session-resume-binding` A named session resumes under the account and directory that hold its transcript

Depends on: `grid-node.accounts-available`, `container.claude-stream-json-contract`

- **Assumes:** sessions/<name>.json maps to a session id whose transcript is <account dir>/projects/<encoded cwd>/<uuid>.jsonl, and the cwd still exists. resolveSession ignores the stored account, so after a rate-limit rotation or a forced account the next --resume runs under a different CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR and should not find the transcript. Transcripts older than cleanupPeriodDays (30) are deleted by Claude Code.
- **Check:** Login node: for each ~/.ashley/sessions/*.json, ls ~/.ashley/accounts/<account>/projects/*/<session_id>.jsonl exists and test -d <cwd>.
- **Looks like:** `No conversation found with session ID: <uuid> (expected wording; capture the exact text on first occurrence)`; `second turn of a session fails right after 'requeued <id> for account <other>'`
- **Repair:** If the transcript sits under another account, resend with that account; if it is gone, rewrite the session entry so the next turn starts a new session id instead of resuming.
- **By hand:** Start a new session name.
- **Could break when:** Claude Code changing its session storage layout or retention.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:166-177,238-245,360-387; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:86-93; docs/RESEARCH.md:114

### `grid-node.account-config-dir-sharing` Concurrent claude processes share one CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR safely

Depends on: `grid-node.accounts-available`

- **Assumes:** Each account directory is the CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR for every run of every worker of that account (concurrency 2 per worker, several workers possible), on the shared Panasas home. Whether Claude Code tolerates concurrent writers to .claude.json/history.jsonl on a network filesystem is not documented either way (the research claim that Anthropic advises against it was refuted).
- **Check:** Login node: running workers x concurrency per account; ls ~/.ashley/accounts/*/ | grep -E 'corrupt|backup' -> none.
- **Looks like:** `a .claude.json.corrupted.* or backup file appears`; `Claude reports its configuration file is corrupted`; `history entries missing`
- **By hand:** Use concurrency 1, or give each worker its own config directory per account.
- **Could break when:** Claude Code storing more state (plugins, memory) in the config dir.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:14-15,31,179-181; docs/RESEARCH.md:34; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:95-99

### `grid-node.worker-preflight` The worker job passes its REFUSING checks

Depends on: `container.ready-record`, `container.runtime-path`, `grid-slurm.output-paths`

- **Assumes:** Before exec, the job sources config.env and refuses unless RUNTIME, SANDBOX, CLAUDE_BIN and ROOT are set, $SANDBOX/usr/local/bin/node and CLAUDE_BIN are executable and RUNTIME exists on this node; it then writes 'node' (hostname -f) and 'job.id'.
- **Check:** Login node: grep REFUSING $(ls -t ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/log/slurm_*.out | head -1) -> nothing; test -s ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/node.
- **Looks like:** `REFUSING: no config.env`; `REFUSING: <VAR> unset in config.env (bootstrap not finished?)`; `REFUSING: sandbox <path> incomplete`; `REFUSING: <path> missing`; `REFUSING: runtime <path> missing on <host>`; `REFUSING: no /dev/shm scratch`
- **Repair:** Follow the named node: missing sandbox/claude -> ashley adopt (shared) or bootstrap --resubmit (own); runtime missing on host -> container.runtime-path repair; then ashley worker start.
- **By hand:** Read the REFUSING line; it names the broken piece.
- **Could break when:** New required config keys added by a later Ashley version while an old config.env remains.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:21-38

### `grid-node.daemon-alive` ashleyd.js is running and heartbeating

Depends on: `grid-node.worker-preflight`, `container.exec-sandbox`, `grid-node.home-quota-headroom`

- **Assumes:** While the worker job is RUNNING, the daemon rewrites state.json every 10 s (alive = mtime younger than 90 s on the login node's clock) and polls the inbox every 250 ms; an exception inside tick is logged and polling continues.
- **Check:** Login node: ashley worker list --json -> job_state RUNNING and alive true; tail -3 ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/log/ashleyd.log shows no 'uncaughtException'.
- **Looks like:** `alive false while job_state RUNNING`; `uncaughtException`; `tick error`; `ashleyd: --worker-dir is required`; `the job COMPLETED seconds after starting`
- **Repair:** ashley worker stop --name <w>; ashley worker start --name <w> (inbox and .claimed requests survive).
- **By hand:** Send ashley worker log --name <w> to the lab admin.
- **Could break when:** Node.js API changes in a newer container Node; an Ashley upgrade left half-applied.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:101-110,112-147,390-411; grid/ashley:165-174

### `grid-node.job-cgroup-resources` The job's CPU, memory and pid limits hold the daemon, claude runs and VS Code

Depends on: `grid-slurm.worker-template-render`

- **Assumes:** 4 CPU / 16G (defaults) cover the daemon, up to CONCURRENCY claude processes and their tools, the /dev/shm scratch (counted as memory), and the Remote-SSH VS Code server that pam_slurm_adopt places into the same job.
- **Check:** Login node: sstat -j $JID.batch -n -o MaxRSS; sacct -j $JID -o MaxRSS,State; grep -c oom-kill ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/log/slurm_$JID.out -> 0.
- **Looks like:** `slurmstepd: error: Detected 1 oom-kill event(s) in StepId=<id>.batch`; `claude exited 137`; `fork: retry: Resource temporarily unavailable`
- **Repair:** After an OOM, restart the worker with --mem doubled (up to 64G) when the queue allows.
- **By hand:** Lower ashley.worker.concurrency or raise ashley.worker.memory.
- **Could break when:** A cgroup v2 migration changes accounting and enforces pids.max.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:11-13; grid/ashleyd.js:31,124; grid/ashley:247; docs/RESEARCH.md:123,131

### `grid-node.home-quota-headroom` The home directory has room for ~/.ashley and ~/.vscode-server

Depends on: `network.grid-login`

- **Assumes:** ~/.ashley (inbox, outbox events, account dirs holding Claude transcripts, sessions, state) and ~/.vscode-server live on the Panasas home (soft quota about 4.3 TB, measured 93 % full). Every state/result write is an atomic .part+rename that fails on EDQUOT, and a failed result write loses that request's answer.
- **Check:** Login node: pan_quota (used < 95 % of soft); du -sh ~/.ashley ~/.vscode-server.
- **Looks like:** `EDQUOT: disk quota exceeded`; `ENOSPC: no space left on device`; `tick error Error: EDQUOT`; `FAIL home quota ...`
- **Repair:** Delete outbox/*.events.jsonl and outbox/*.json older than 30 days; prune old VS Code server builds (grid-node.vscode-server-install). Never delete transcripts automatically.
- **By hand:** Move large data to the lab volume.
- **Could break when:** Quota policy changes; transcripts growing under the account dirs.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:158-162,239; grid/ashleyd.js:41-45,194-195,460-463; docs/LIMITS.md:49

### `grid-node.shared-home-coherence` Login and compute nodes see one consistent ~/.ashley

Depends on: `grid-node.daemon-alive`

- **Assumes:** The relay on the login node writes inbox files (.part then rename) and the daemon on a compute node sees them in readdir within one 250 ms poll; rename-to-.claimed is atomic across clients; events.jsonl appends made on the node are visible to stat/tail on the login node. Measured receipt 0.19 s median.
- **Check:** Login node: node-ashley-cli latency 5 (from the laptop) -> receipt under 1 s; or write ~/.ashley/workers/<w>/probe.$$ on login and PROBE(ls) it from the node within 1 s.
- **Looks like:** `'queued <id>' but no events for minutes while the worker is idle`; `receipt latency far above 1 s`; `the same id claimed twice`
- **By hand:** Report to the lab admin; lengthen the poll.
- **Could break when:** A home migration off Panasas to an NFS-style store with attribute caching would break the 250 ms assumption.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:32-34,119-131,460-463; grid/ashley:310-316,400-407,417-425; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:34-84

### `grid-node.clock-sync` Node, login node and file server clocks agree

Depends on: `grid-node.daemon-alive`

- **Assumes:** Liveness compares state.json's mtime with the login node's clock (90 s), the peer note compares last_beat with the node's clock, and cooldowns are ISO times; a skew of more than a few seconds misreports liveness.
- **Check:** Login node: date +%s versus PROBE(date +%s) -> difference < 5 s.
- **Looks like:** `worker RUNNING but shown as 'starting' forever`; `live peers missing from the peer note`
- **By hand:** Report the skewed node to hpc@wayne.edu.
- **Could break when:** NTP/chrony configuration changes at an OS upgrade.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:169-171; grid/ashleyd.js:297,390-411; extension/src/vscode/tree.ts:93-97

### `grid-node.daemon-script-current` Running workers execute the installed ashleyd.js

Depends on: `grid-node.daemon-alive`, `grid-login.ashley-scripts-installed`

- **Assumes:** A worker keeps the daemon code it started with; the wizard uploads new scripts (VERSION 2) without restarting workers, and state.json carries no daemon version, only claude_version.
- **Check:** Login node: stat -c %Y ~/.ashley/bin/ashleyd.js is older than the worker's state.json 'started' time; cat ~/.ashley/bin/VERSION equals the extension's dist/grid/VERSION.
- **Looks like:** `the grid has Ashley v<old>; this extension brings v<new>`; `behaviour predates the docs (1 s poll, no peer note)`
- **Repair:** When the worker is idle (running [] and queued 0): ashley worker stop then start.
- **By hand:** Restart the worker after updating.
- **Could break when:** Every Ashley release.
- **Code:** grid/VERSION:1; grid/ashleyd.js:99-102; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:492-510,529-535

### `grid-node.secrets-env-loaded-at-start` The worker has the current GitHub and Hugging Face tokens

Depends on: `grid-node.daemon-alive`, `grid-login.secrets-env`

- **Assumes:** ~/.ashley/secrets.env is read once when the daemon starts and merged into every claude environment; renewing a token rewrites the file, but a running worker keeps the old value until it restarts (Claude account token files, by contrast, are re-read per request).
- **Check:** Login node: stat -c %Y ~/.ashley/secrets.env is older than the worker's 'started' time.
- **Looks like:** `remote: Invalid username or token`; `401 Client Error ... Invalid user token (hf)`; `Authentication failed for 'https://github.com/...'`
- **Repair:** Restart idle workers after pushTokens.
- **By hand:** ashley worker stop and start after changing tokens.
- **Could break when:** Token rotations becoming more frequent (fine-grained PAT expiry).
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:96,179; extension/src/core/wizard.ts:472-480

### `grid-node.workspace-root` Session working directories exist and are visible inside the container

Depends on: `container.binds`, `grid-node.root-storage`

- **Assumes:** The daemon gets ASHLEY_ROOT=$ROOT; a new session defaults to $ROOT/workspaces/<session>; a request cwd (e.g. ~/Student_Projects/...) must lie under a bound path, since mkdirSync happens inside the container.
- **Check:** Compute node: CEXEC(bash -c 'test -w "$ROOT/workspaces" && echo ok') and test -d for each session cwd in ~/.ashley/sessions/*.json.
- **Looks like:** `tick error Error: ENOENT: no such file or directory, mkdir '<path>'`; `EACCES: permission denied, mkdir`
- **Repair:** If the path exists on the host but not in the container, add its top directory to BINDS and restart the worker.
- **By hand:** Use a cwd under home or the lab volume.
- **Could break when:** New storage locations outside the bound set.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:36,166-168,371-376; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:46

### `grid-node.prompt-argv-limit` Prompts fit in one argv element

Depends on: `grid-node.daemon-alive`

- **Assumes:** The prompt and the appended system prompt are passed as command-line arguments; Linux limits one argument to 128 KiB (MAX_ARG_STRLEN). A larger prompt fails at spawn.
- **Check:** Login node: wc -c ~/.ashley/workers/*/inbox/*.json -> each under 100 KiB (laptop side: refuse or spill larger prompts to a file).
- **Looks like:** `spawn E2BIG`; `request left as <id>.json.claimed with no outbox result`
- **By hand:** Put long material in a file on the grid and reference it in the prompt.
- **Could break when:** Pasting whole documents into prompts as usage grows.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:169,175,199-200

### `grid-node.run-hard-cap` A claude run ends by itself instead of waiting on a permission prompt

Depends on: `container.claude-cli-contract`

- **Assumes:** In -p mode with --permission-mode acceptEdits, an action needing approval is expected to be denied rather than waited on; --permission-prompts none (2.1.259+, documented to prevent hangs) is not passed. A hung run holds a concurrency slot until the 6 h hard cap.
- **Check:** Login node: for ids in state.json 'running', the matching outbox/<id>.events.jsonl was modified within the last 30 minutes.
- **Looks like:** `hard cap reached for <id>; killing`; `status cancelled after about 6 h`; `running ids whose events file stopped growing`
- **By hand:** Restart the worker to free the slot; add --permission-prompts none to the daemon's argv.
- **Could break when:** Permission behaviour of -p changing between versions.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:46,170-171,219-222; docs/RESEARCH.md:38

### `grid-node.hostkeys-stable` Compute-node host keys match the laptop's known_hosts

Depends on: `laptop.openssh`, `laptop.ssh-config-block`

- **Assumes:** The node block uses StrictHostKeyChecking accept-new: new nodes are learned silently, but a reinstalled node with new keys hard-fails.
- **Check:** Laptop: ssh -o BatchMode=yes <node> true does not print 'REMOTE HOST IDENTIFICATION HAS CHANGED'; compare with ssh wsu-grid ssh-keyscan -t ed25519 <node>.
- **Looks like:** `WARNING: REMOTE HOST IDENTIFICATION HAS CHANGED!`; `Host key verification failed.`
- **Repair:** Only when the key the login node sees for that node equals the new key: ssh-keygen -R <node> on the laptop, then reconnect.
- **By hand:** Confirm the reinstall with C&IT before removing the old key.
- **Could break when:** The el7 -> el9 reinstall regenerates every node's host keys at once.
- **Code:** extension/src/core/sshConfig.ts:48-58

### `grid-node.vscode-bashrc-exports` The sysroot variables are exported when Remote-SSH starts the server

Depends on: `grid-login.bashrc-block`, `grid-node.vscode-sysroot`

- **Assumes:** Remote-SSH's non-interactive ssh sources ~/.bashrc (Red Hat bash with SSH_SOURCE_BASHRC) and the ashley block sits before any interactive-only 'return' guard, so VSCODE_SERVER_CUSTOM_GLIBC_LINKER/PATH and VSCODE_SERVER_PATCHELF_PATH are set.
- **Check:** Laptop: ssh -o BatchMode=yes wsu-grid 'echo ${VSCODE_SERVER_CUSTOM_GLIBC_LINKER:-UNSET}' -> the sysroot path (same home and bash as the nodes); login node: the line number of '# >>> ashley' in ~/.bashrc is below the first top-level 'return'.
- **Looks like:** `UNSET`; `exit code 207`; `glibc`; `You are connecting to an OS version that is unsupported by Visual Studio Code (followed by failure)`
- **Repair:** ashley install rewrites the block; if a return guard precedes it, move the block above the guard (keep a backup of ~/.bashrc).
- **By hand:** Move the '# >>> ashley' block above '[ -z "$PS1" ] && return'.
- **Could break when:** WSU changing /etc/bashrc or the skeleton; VS Code changing which shell it launches.
- **Code:** grid/bashrc.block:1-10; grid/ashley:117-124,232; docs/manual/90_troubleshooting.md:22; docs/RESEARCH.md:273-274

### `grid-node.vscode-server-install` The VS Code server installs and runs from ~/.vscode-server

Depends on: `grid-node.vscode-bashrc-exports`, `grid-node.home-quota-headroom`, `grid-node.egress`

- **Assumes:** With no remote.SSH.serverInstallPath, the server goes to ~/.vscode-server on the nearly full home; the node downloads the build matching the laptop's VS Code commit from Microsoft's CDN; each VS Code release adds another build.
- **Check:** Compute node: PROBE(curl -s -m 10 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' https://update.code.visualstudio.com/api/latest/server-linux-x64/stable) -> 302; login node: ls ~/.vscode-server/cli/servers; du -sh ~/.vscode-server; the newest log.txt shows 'Extension host agent started'.
- **Looks like:** `Failed to download VS Code Server`; `EDQUOT: disk quota exceeded`; `tar: ... Cannot write`
- **Repair:** Delete ~/.vscode-server/cli/servers/Stable-<commit> for all but the two newest commits, only when no running job node has a process from that commit.
- **By hand:** Remote-SSH: Kill VS Code Server on Host, then reconnect.
- **Could break when:** Microsoft changing download hosts or layout; home quota.
- **Code:** docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:253; docs/RESEARCH.md:268

### `grid-node.vscode-stale-server` No stale VS Code server blocks a new connection

Depends on: `grid-node.vscode-server-install`

- **Assumes:** A server process left from an earlier window coincided with two 302 s timeouts on asx1 (not diagnosed).
- **Check:** Compute node, in the worker allocation: PROBE(pgrep -u $USER -fc '.vscode-server/cli/servers') compared with the number of open Remote windows for that node.
- **Looks like:** `two consecutive 'Connecting with SSH timed out' at about 302 s while the server log shows the previous connection`
- **Repair:** PROBE(pkill -u $USER -f '.vscode-server/cli/servers') on that node, then reconnect.
- **By hand:** Command Palette: Remote-SSH: Kill VS Code Server on Host.
- **Could break when:** Remote-SSH exec-server behaviour changes.
- **Code:** docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:253; docs/manual/90_troubleshooting.md:20

### `grid-node.vscode-runtime-sockets` VS Code server sockets survive the node's /tmp cleaner (unverified)

Depends on: `grid-node.vscode-server-install`

- **Assumes:** Under pam_slurm_adopt, pam_systemd is disabled and XDG_RUNTIME_DIR is unset, so the server's IPC and terminal sockets probably live in the node's /tmp, which a cleaner sweeps while jobs run (it deleted multiprocessing sockets of other jobs). Not observed yet for VS Code.
- **Check:** Laptop: ssh <node> 'echo ${XDG_RUNTIME_DIR:-UNSET}; ls /tmp | grep -c vscode'.
- **Looks like:** `The terminal process failed to launch`; `ENOENT ... vscode-ipc-*.sock`; `extension host reconnect loops after hours`
- **By hand:** Reload the window.
- **Could break when:** Cleaner policy changes.
- **Code:** docs/RESEARCH.md:131; CLAUDE.md (Grid: /tmp swept)

### `node.dev-shm-capacity` /dev/shm scratch on the node

Depends on: `grid.worker`

- **Assumes:** /dev/shm is writable with room to spare. It is bound as /tmp in the container, and what the job writes there counts against the job's --mem (16G). Scratch left by SIGKILLed jobs, whose trap never ran, stays behind.
- **Check:** grid (in the worker via srun --overlap): `df -k /dev/shm` free > 2 GB. List /dev/shm/${USER}_ashley_* directories whose job id is no longer running.
- **Looks like:** `REFUSING: no /dev/shm scratch`; `ENOSPC on /tmp inside the container`; `oom-kill event in slurm_*.out`
- **Repair:** Remove ${USER}_ashley_* and ${USER}_diag_* directories of jobs that squeue no longer lists.
- **By hand:** Raise worker memory, or keep large temp files in ROOT.
- **Could break when:** Node tmpfs sizing or cgroup accounting changes.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:39-41; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:42; grid/ashley:817

### `node.user-namespaces` Apptainer exec mode on the node

Depends on: `grid.runtime`

- **Assumes:** The configured apptainer can exec a sandbox without root. Either it is installed setuid, or unprivileged user namespaces are enabled (user.max_user_namespaces > 0).
- **Check:** grid (in the worker): `cat /proc/sys/user/max_user_namespaces` > 0, or `ls -l $(dirname $RUNTIME)/../libexec/apptainer/bin/starter-suid` is setuid.
- **Looks like:** `FATAL: while performing image mount: ... user namespace not supported`; `starter not found`; `Operation not permitted`
- **By hand:** Report to hpc@wayne.edu.
- **Could break when:** A kernel CVE response disabling user namespaces, or a change in apptainer install mode.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:45-47; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:74-81

### `node.bind-sources-exist` Bind sources exist on the node

Depends on: `lab.cephfs-mounted`, `grid.worker`

- **Assumes:** Every BINDS source exists on the compute node: $HOME, /rs/rs_grp_oschome/$USER, the shared lab folder and ROOT. Autofs may mount CephFS only on first access.
- **Check:** grid (in the worker): for each -B path in config.env BINDS, `ls -d <path>` succeeds.
- **Looks like:** `FATAL: container creation failed: mount hook function failure: mount /rs/rs_grp_oschome/shared->... error: no such file or directory`; `REFUSING: sandbox ... incomplete`
- **Repair:** Remove bind entries whose source no longer exists from config.env (set_cfg BINDS) and restart the worker.
- **By hand:** Ask the lead whether the lab volume moved.
- **Could break when:** CephFS path or mount changes.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:97-100; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:60-62; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:31

### `node.egress-endpoints` Node reaches every service it needs

Depends on: `node.probe`

- **Assumes:** Compute nodes have direct outbound HTTPS, with no proxy, to api.anthropic.com, claude.ai/claude.com and platform.claude.com (OAuth and Remote Control), registry.npmjs.org, registry-1.docker.io, huggingface.co and github.com. If a proxy is introduced, HTTPS_PROXY must pass into the container.
- **Check:** grid (in the worker, inside the container): curl -s -m 10 -o /dev/null -w %{http_code} for each host. Pass = not 000.
- **Looks like:** `000 (curl timeout)`; `getaddrinfo EAI_AGAIN`; `Remote Control stays 'starting'`; `npm ERR! network`
- **By hand:** Report to hpc@wayne.edu. If a proxy is announced, add HTTPS_PROXY to secrets.env.
- **Could break when:** C&IT adding an egress firewall or proxy to compute nodes.
- **Code:** grid/ashley:819; grid/bootstrap.sbatch:90,95; grid/phone.js:11

### `node.gpu-nv` GPU visible inside the container

Depends on: `grid.gpu-qos-gres`, `node.probe`

- **Assumes:** For GPU workers, `--nv` binds the host NVIDIA driver libraries and the container image's CUDA works with the node's driver.
- **Check:** grid (in a GPU worker): `$RUNTIME exec --nv $SANDBOX nvidia-smi -L` lists one GPU. Skip for CPU workers.
- **Looks like:** `WARNING: Could not find any nv files on this host!`; `CUDA driver version is insufficient for CUDA runtime version`
- **By hand:** Lead publishes an image that matches the node driver.
- **Could break when:** Driver upgrades on GPU nodes, CUDA image upgrades.
- **Code:** grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:42-47

### `node.vscode-server-glibc` VS Code server runs on the node with the C&IT sysroot

Depends on: `grid.vscode-sysroot`, `grid.bashrc-effective-noninteractive`

- **Assumes:** The C&IT sysroot's glibc (via VSCODE_SERVER_CUSTOM_GLIBC_LINKER/PATH and patchelf 0.19.1) meets the current VS Code server's glibc floor. VS Code keeps this unsupported escape hatch.
- **Check:** grid (in the worker): the sysroot's libc.so.6 version is >= the floor stated for the laptop's VS Code version. The newest ~/.vscode-server/cli/servers/*/log.txt contains 'Extension host agent started' after the last connect.
- **Looks like:** `exit code 207`; `GLIBC_2.28 not found`; `The remote host may not meet VS Code Server's prerequisites`
- **By hand:** Ask hpc@wayne.edu for an updated sysroot. Meanwhile pin the previous VS Code version.
- **Could break when:** VS Code raising the glibc floor or removing the custom-glibc variables. C&IT not updating the sysroot. A grid OS upgrade making it unnecessary.
- **Code:** grid/bashrc.block:1-9; grid/ashley:694-706

### `node.stale-vscode-server` No stale VS Code server blocking a reconnect

Depends on: `network.node-proxyjump`

- **Assumes:** A server left over from an earlier window on the node does not block a new connection. It was observed: two 302 s timeouts while pid 25363 still ran.
- **Check:** grid (in the worker): `pgrep -u $USER -f vscode-server` older than the last successful connect, and more than one server commit running.
- **Looks like:** `Connecting with SSH timed out (302 s) while an earlier window's server still runs`
- **Repair:** `pkill -u $USER -f 'vscode-server/cli/servers/Stable-<old commit>'` in the worker allocation.
- **By hand:** Remote-SSH: Kill VS Code Server on Host, then reconnect.
- **Could break when:** Remote-SSH reconnect logic changes.
- **Code:** docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:253

### `node.claude-config-integrity` Account .claude.json intact

Depends on: `grid.accounts`

- **Assumes:** Account config dirs sit on the network home (Anthropic advises against this). Concurrent claude processes in one CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR (concurrency 2, several workers) must not corrupt .claude.json. Its lock is mkdir-based and goes stale after 10 s.
- **Check:** grid: each ~/.ashley/accounts/*/.claude.json and remote/config/.claude.json parses as JSON.
- **Looks like:** `Configuration file at ... is corrupted`; `SyntaxError: Unexpected end of JSON input`
- **Repair:** Restore the newest .claude.json.backup* that parses, or remove the file (it is recreated; the phone config is reseeded by pretrust).
- **By hand:** Lower worker concurrency to 1 if it recurs.
- **Could break when:** Claude Code config-write changes on network filesystems.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:187-201,326-346; grid/phone.js:100-117

## Container (23)

### `container.runtime-path` The recorded RUNTIME path runs on every compute node without 'module load'

Depends on: `grid-node.module-system`

- **Assumes:** RUNTIME is stored as an absolute path (config.env, and library.json 'runtime' for shared containers) and the worker executes it directly, without loading the module's environment; the path must exist and work on whichever node Slurm picks.
- **Check:** Compute node: PROBE(bash -c 'source ~/.ashley/config.env; test -x "$RUNTIME" && "$RUNTIME" --version') -> 'apptainer version 1.2.2'.
- **Looks like:** `REFUSING: runtime <path> missing on <host>`; `exit 127`; `apptainer: error while loading shared libraries`
- **Repair:** Own container: ashley bootstrap --resubmit (the sandbox exists, so only discovery and the config rewrite run). Shared container: admin republishes the entry (or patches library.json 'runtime'), then ashley adopt <entry> for each student.
- **By hand:** Tell the lab admin the runtime path in library.json no longer exists.
- **Could break when:** The path embeds the OS and compiler (/wsu/el7/gnu7/...), so an OS upgrade breaks every stored RUNTIME at once.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:50,77-81,125; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:35,45; grid/ashley:90,100; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:154

### `container.image-tag-drift` The base image tag is stable enough, and Node 22 is supported

Depends on: `external.docker-hub`

- **Assumes:** docker://node:22-bookworm is a moving tag: a rebuild months later gives newer Node 22.x and Debian packages than the entry recorded. Node 22 reaches end of life on 2027-04-30; bookworm's glibc 2.36 stays above Claude Code's floor.
- **Check:** Compute node: CEXEC(bash -c 'node -v; ldd --version | head -1') -> v22.x and glibc 2.36; login node: the library.json entry's 'image' and 'built' fields.
- **Looks like:** `a republished entry behaves differently from the old one`; `npm WARN EBADENGINE`
- **By hand:** Pin by digest when publishing (docker://node:22-bookworm@sha256:<digest>) and record the digest; before April 2027 publish a node:24 entry and make it the default.
- **Could break when:** Node 22 EOL; Debian bookworm moving to LTS; Claude Code raising its Node or glibc floor.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:33; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:130-135; docs/INSTALL.md:93-95

### `container.sandbox-build` apptainer build --sandbox works unprivileged on a compute node

Depends on: `container.runtime-path`, `grid-node.user-namespaces`, `grid-node.root-storage`, `external.docker-hub`

- **Assumes:** A sandbox directory (not a SIF: SIF-to-sandbox conversion failed on this grid and some nodes lack unsquashfs) is built into $SANDBOX.part with APPTAINER_CACHEDIR/TMPDIR on ROOT, checked for /usr/local/bin/node, then moved into place. Measured: 6-7 min, 1.1 GB.
- **Check:** Login node: source ~/.ashley/config.env; test -x "$SANDBOX/usr/local/bin/node" && test ! -e "$SANDBOX.part"; the bootstrap log contains 'built <size>' or 'sandbox exists'.
- **Looks like:** `build with <runtime> failed:`; `FATAL: While performing build:`; `INFO: Converting SIF file to temporary sandbox...`; `no space left on device`
- **Repair:** rm -rf "$SANDBOX.part"; clear $ROOT/cache in a job; resubmit the bootstrap.
- **By hand:** Admin only; students use the lab container.
- **Could break when:** Apptainer releases change unprivileged build behaviour; CephFS quota; many small files on CephFS.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:41-43,64-73; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:130-135; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:242,267

### `container.image-contents` The image carries Node at /usr/local/bin/node, npm, bash, git, curl and python3

Depends on: `container.sandbox-build`

- **Assumes:** /usr/local/bin/node is the readiness marker everywhere (build, exec test, worker preflight, adopt); npm installs Claude Code; python3 merges library.json for LIBRARY=1; git/curl are in the smoke test; ashleyd.js needs Node >= 16.7 (crypto.randomUUID, fs.rmSync). Images without Node at that path (INSTALL.md's nvidia/cuda example) fail the marker, every runtime candidate rebuilds, and the job ends 'no runtime could exec'. A python3-less image skips the library merge unchecked yet still prints 'published'.
- **Check:** Compute node: CEXEC(bash -c 'for b in node npm bash git curl python3; do command -v $b >/dev/null || echo MISSING $b; done; test -x /usr/local/bin/node || echo MISSING marker') -> no output.
- **Looks like:** `build with <runtime> failed:`; `STOP: sandbox built but no runtime could exec it`; `python3: not found`; `'published <name>' in the log without 'library entry written: <name>'`
- **By hand:** Base GPU and R entries on an image that installs Node 22 at /usr/local/bin (or add it in a definition file) and has python3.
- **Could break when:** The official node image changing its layout or dropping python3/git in slimmer variants.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:66,69,75,95,102-121; docs/INSTALL.md:90,94; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:33; grid/ashley:92,274

### `container.ready-record` READY and config.env describe a usable container

Depends on: `grid-slurm.bootstrap-job`, `lab-folder.library-entry`

- **Assumes:** Satisfied by EITHER path (follow the one config.env SOURCE names): own build (bootstrap writes RUNTIME, SANDBOX, CLAUDE_BIN, BINDS, BOOTSTRAP_STATE=done and READY) or adoption (ashley adopt writes the same keys from library.json, SOURCE=shared). is_ready requires READY non-empty, CLAUDE_BIN executable and $SANDBOX/usr/local/bin/node executable.
- **Check:** Login node: ashley status --json -> ready true; grep -cE '^(ROOT|RUNTIME|SANDBOX|CLAUDE_BIN|BINDS)=' ~/.ashley/config.env -> 5.
- **Looks like:** `FAIL not ready (bootstrap job <id>: <state>)`; `grid not ready yet (bootstrap job ...)`; `no container yet: it comes with lab access`; `REFUSING: <VAR> unset in config.env (bootstrap not finished?)`
- **Repair:** If the library is readable: ashley adopt <default>; admin with no library: ashley bootstrap --resubmit.
- **By hand:** Students: wait for lab access (the wizard's last step). Admin: read the bootstrap log.
- **Could break when:** A stale BOOTSTRAP_JOB can block the awaiting-access path (access review finding).
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:123-133; grid/ashley:30-31,85-103,126-136,205,258-260

### `container.binds` The container sees home, the CephFS tree, ROOT and the lab folder

Depends on: `container.ready-record`, `grid-node.root-storage`

- **Assumes:** BINDS holds -B $HOME, -B /rs/rs_grp_oschome/$USER (if it exists), -B $ROOT when outside those, and -B /rs/rs_grp_oschome/shared for adopted or library sandboxes (added again by the template when the sandbox lives there). Binding the parent '-B /rs' does NOT expose the tree (/rs is a symlink into /.rs/volumes); each path is bound explicitly. BINDS is word-split, so paths must not contain spaces.
- **Check:** Compute node: CEXEC(bash -c 'for p in "$HOME" "$ROOT" /rs/rs_grp_oschome/shared "$(dirname $CLAUDE_BIN)"; do test -r "$p" || echo MISSING $p; done') -> no output; login node: grep ^BINDS= ~/.ashley/config.env.
- **Looks like:** `npm error code ENOENT (on the shared path)`; `No such file or directory (for a file that exists on the host)`; `cannot open file`
- **Repair:** Rerun ashley adopt <entry> (recomputes BINDS including the lab folder) or append the missing -B to BINDS in config.env; restart the worker.
- **By hand:** Tell the lab admin which host path is missing inside the worker.
- **Could break when:** New lab storage (another volume, datasets elsewhere) needs a bind that nothing adds automatically; an OS upgrade moving /rs.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:60-62,84; grid/ashley:97-99; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:28-31,44-45; docs/research/access_review_2026-09-28.json (worker.sbatch.tpl:31 finding)

### `container.exec-sandbox` apptainer exec runs Node inside the sandbox as the user

Depends on: `container.ready-record`, `container.binds`, `grid-node.dev-shm-scratch`, `grid-node.user-namespaces`

- **Assumes:** $RUNTIME exec $BINDS -B <shm>:/tmp $SANDBOX node ... prints 'node v22...' on a compute node, without SIF conversion; uid inside equals the user's (non-root) and apptainer's adapted /etc/passwd has the user (os.userInfo() in the daemon's peer note throws otherwise, leaving the request stuck as .claimed).
- **Check:** Compute node: CEXEC(bash -c 'node -v; id -u; getent passwd $(id -u) | cut -d: -f1') -> v22.x, a non-zero uid, the AccessID.
- **Looks like:** `runtime <rt> cannot exec: <output>`; `FATAL: while executing starter: /usr/libexec/singularity/bin/starter not found`; `INFO: Converting SIF file to temporary sandbox...`; `exit 127`; `uv_os_get_passwd returned ENOENT`
- **Repair:** ashley bootstrap --resubmit (rediscovers a runtime that can exec) or ashley adopt for shared containers.
- **By hand:** Report the runtime/node to the lab admin.
- **Could break when:** Apptainer config changes (config passwd = no, mount home = no), a switch from userns to setuid or vice versa.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:46,74-81; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:45-47; grid/ashleyd.js:291-308

### `container.env-isolation` Only intended environment reaches Claude inside the container

Depends on: `container.exec-sandbox`

- **Assumes:** sbatch exports the submitting ssh session's environment (which sourced ~/.bashrc) and apptainer passes host variables into the container (PATH is reset). The daemon deletes CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN and ANTHROPIC_API_KEY and sets CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR, DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER, CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC, DISABLE_TELEMETRY; other routing variables (ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL, ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN, CLAUDE_CODE_USE_BEDROCK/VERTEX, HTTPS_PROXY, NODE_OPTIONS, APPTAINERENV_*) are not removed and would change where requests go or how Node runs.
- **Check:** Compute node, inside the worker allocation: PROBE(bash -c 'env | cut -d= -f1 | grep -E "^(ANTHROPIC_|CLAUDE_CODE_USE_|NODE_OPTIONS|HTTPS?_PROXY|APPTAINERENV_|SINGULARITYENV_)"') -> empty (names only; values are never printed).
- **Looks like:** `API Error: 401 against a non-Anthropic base URL`; `Could not load credentials from any providers`; `usage billed to an unexpected provider`
- **By hand:** Remove those exports from ~/.bashrc (or guard them), then restart the worker; long term the daemon should delete them too.
- **Could break when:** Claude Code adds new env knobs that override auth or endpoints.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:179-192; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:45-47; grid/ashley:152,281

### `container.dns-tls-egress` DNS and TLS work inside the container

Depends on: `container.exec-sandbox`, `grid-node.egress`

- **Assumes:** The container uses apptainer's adapted /etc/resolv.conf and Debian bookworm's CA bundle; no TLS interception and no proxy are needed for api.anthropic.com, registry.npmjs.org, github.com and huggingface.co.
- **Check:** Compute node: CEXEC(bash -c 'for u in api.anthropic.com registry.npmjs.org github.com huggingface.co; do printf "%s " $u; curl -s -m 8 -o /dev/null -w "%{http_code}\n" https://$u/; done') -> no 000.
- **Looks like:** `curl: (6) Could not resolve host`; `curl: (60) SSL certificate problem: unable to get local issuer certificate`; `UNABLE_TO_GET_ISSUER_CERT_LOCALLY`; `smoke: ... net=000`
- **By hand:** If C&IT adds TLS inspection, set NODE_EXTRA_CA_CERTS and SSL_CERT_FILE for the worker; if a proxy appears, HTTPS_PROXY.
- **Could break when:** CA rotations make an old, never-rebuilt sandbox's bundle stale; campus network policy changes.
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:95-96; docs/LIMITS.md:53

### `container.claude-installed` The pinned Claude Code binary is installed and executable

Depends on: `external.npm-registry`, `container.exec-sandbox`

- **Assumes:** $NPM_PREFIX/bin/claude exists, is executable by the user (group/ACL X for shared entries), and 'claude --version' prints '2.1.283 (Claude Code)'. The bootstrap's idempotency compares the first field with CLAUDE_CODE_VERSION; if the output format changes it reinstalls every run.
- **Check:** Compute node: CEXEC(bash -c 'source ~/.ashley/config.env; $CLAUDE_BIN --version') -> '2.1.283 (Claude Code)'; login node: claude_version in ~/.ashley/READY and in each worker's state.json agree.
- **Looks like:** `REFUSING: <path>/bin/claude missing`; `STOP: <path>/bin/claude missing after install`; `Permission denied`; `claude_version differs between READY and state.json`
- **Repair:** Own container: ashley bootstrap --update (UPDATE=1 reinstalls at the pin). Shared: admin publishes a new entry; students ashley adopt.
- **By hand:** Admin republishes.
- **Could break when:** Each Claude Code release; npm packaging changes (native binary delivery).
- **Code:** grid/bootstrap.sbatch:83-94; grid/ashleyd.js:91-95; grid/worker.sbatch.tpl:34

### `container.claude-glibc-floor` The container's glibc satisfies Claude Code's Bun binary

Depends on: `container.claude-installed`

- **Assumes:** Claude Code is a Bun native ELF; since about 2.1.243 it needs GLIBC_2.26+ and it has crashed on CentOS 7.9's glibc 2.17, so it runs only inside the container (bookworm, glibc 2.36), never on the host.
- **Check:** Compute node: CEXEC(bash -c 'ldd --version | head -1; objdump -T $(readlink -f $CLAUDE_BIN) 2>/dev/null | grep -o "GLIBC_[0-9.]*" | sort -uV | tail -1') -> 2.36 and a required symbol version <= 2.36.
- **Looks like:** `version GLIBC_2.xx' not found`; `Illegal instruction (core dumped)`
- **By hand:** Publish an entry from a newer base image (Debian trixie / Ubuntu 24.04 with Node 22+).
- **Could break when:** A future Claude Code build requiring glibc newer than bookworm's 2.36.
- **Code:** docs/RESEARCH.md:98,108-110; docs/ARCHITECTURE.md:133-135

### `container.claude-autoupdate-off` Claude Code does not update itself or write into the shared prefix

Depends on: `container.claude-installed`

- **Assumes:** DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1 and CLAUDE_CODE_DISABLE_NONESSENTIAL_TRAFFIC=1 keep the pinned version (so every student runs the same bits) and avoid writes into the read-only shared npm prefix.
- **Check:** Login node: claude_version in state.json of every worker equals the entry's claude_version in library.json/READY across restarts.
- **Looks like:** `claude_version drifts between workers`; `EACCES: permission denied (writing under the npm prefix)`
- **Repair:** ashley bootstrap --update for own containers.
- **By hand:** Admin republishes the entry.
- **Could break when:** Claude Code renaming these variables (DISABLE_UPDATES was added alongside).
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:179-184; docs/RESEARCH.md:116

### `container.claude-cli-contract` The pinned claude accepts every flag the daemon passes

Depends on: `container.claude-installed`, `container.claude-glibc-floor`, `grid-node.kernel-cpu`

- **Assumes:** argv = -p <prompt> --output-format stream-json --verbose (--dangerously-skip-permissions | --permission-mode <mode>) --max-turns N [--model M] [--allowedTools a,b] --append-system-prompt <note> (--resume <uuid> | --session-id <uuid>). stream-json with --print requires --verbose (verified on 2.1.283). --bare must never be added (it ignores CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN). --dangerously-skip-permissions is refused as root.
- **Check:** Compute node: CEXEC($CLAUDE_BIN --help) contains --output-format, --verbose, --permission-mode, --dangerously-skip-permissions, --model, --allowedTools, --append-system-prompt, --resume, --session-id; hidden flags (--max-turns) are proven by the zero-cost canary in container.claude-stream-json-contract not printing 'unknown option'.
- **Looks like:** `error: unknown option '--max-turns'`; `Error: When using --print, --output-format=stream-json requires --verbose`; `--dangerously-skip-permissions cannot be used with root/sudo privileges for security reasons`; `Error: Invalid session ID`
- **Repair:** Reinstall the last pin that passed (bootstrap UPDATE=1 CLAUDE_CODE_VERSION=<previous>).
- **By hand:** Update ashleyd.js argv to the renamed flags and republish.
- **Could break when:** Flag renames or removals in Claude Code; --bare becoming the -p default (the docs call it the recommended script mode) would silently stop subscription auth.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:169-177; docs/RESEARCH.md:36,118

### `container.claude-stream-json-contract` claude -p emits NDJSON ending in a result event the daemon parses

Depends on: `container.claude-cli-contract`

- **Assumes:** Each stdout line is JSON; the turn's last is {type:'result', is_error, subtype, result, session_id, total_cost_usd, duration_ms, num_turns, usage}. A result arrives even on auth failure (is_error true). The error arm carries errors[] and may lack 'result'; the daemon reports only 'subtype', so the cause is lost.
- **Check:** Compute node, zero cost (no valid token used): CEXEC(bash -c 'CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=$(mktemp -d) CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN=sk-ant-oat01-invalid $CLAUDE_BIN -p ping --output-format stream-json --verbose --max-turns 1 | tail -1') -> JSON with "type":"result" and "is_error":true. Laptop: npm run test:unit passes (daemon.test.ts with fake-claude.js).
- **Looks like:** `claude exited <code> without a result: <stderr>`; `status error with empty result on every request`; `result error 'error_during_execution' with no further detail`
- **Repair:** Reinstall the last pin that passed the canary.
- **By hand:** Adapt ashleyd.js parsing; include result.errors in the error text.
- **Could break when:** Anthropic changes stream-json event names or fields (cost, usage) in any release.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:204-214,223-276; extension/test-fixtures/fake-claude.js:21-32; extension/src/core/daemon.test.ts:1-60; docs/CHECKPOINT_2026-09-28.md:294; docs/RESEARCH.md:15

### `container.claude-shell-snapshot` Claude's Bash tool can start a login shell inside the container

Depends on: `container.exec-sandbox`, `grid-login.bashrc-block`

- **Assumes:** Claude's Bash tool snapshots a login shell that sources ~/.bashrc inside the container. The student's bashrc (module calls, conda hooks, /etc/profile.d/modules.sh, which the container lacks) must not error or hang; a public issue (#42952) saw every Bash command fail under HPC cgroups, Apptainer and Lmod. The ashley block adds ~/.ashley/bin to PATH, which the peer note relies on.
- **Check:** Compute node: CEXEC(env -i HOME=$HOME TERM=dumb bash -lc 'echo SNAPSHOT_OK; command -v ashley') -> SNAPSHOT_OK and ~/.ashley/bin/ashley within 5 s, nothing on stderr.
- **Looks like:** `bash: module: command not found`; `/etc/profile.d/modules.sh: No such file or directory`; `Bash tool calls failing or timing out on every command`
- **By hand:** Guard HPC-only lines in ~/.bashrc with '[ -z "$APPTAINER_CONTAINER" ]' or '[ -f /etc/profile.d/modules.sh ]'; set CLAUDE_CODE_SHELL=/bin/bash.
- **Could break when:** Claude Code changing its snapshot mechanism; WSU changing the default bashrc skeleton.
- **Code:** docs/RESEARCH.md:123; grid/bashrc.block:8-9; grid/ashleyd.js:303

### `container.slurm-clients-inside` Slurm commands inside the worker container (currently not provided)

Depends on: `container.exec-sandbox`

- **Assumes:** LIMITS.md says the worker Claude can submit and poll jobs, and the peer note tells it to 'ashley worker start'. Nothing binds sbatch/squeue/scancel, /etc/slurm or the munge socket into the container, so from Claude's shell they should be missing. 'ashley send' still works because job_state swallows the missing squeue. Not tested from inside a worker.
- **Check:** Compute node: CEXEC(bash -c 'command -v sbatch squeue scancel || echo MISSING') -> today expected MISSING; pass once bound.
- **Looks like:** `bash: sbatch: command not found`; `ashley: sbatch failed: ... command not found`; `queued <id> on worker <w> (job ... not running)`
- **By hand:** Bind the site's Slurm client binaries, libraries, /etc/slurm and /var/run/munge into the container (site-specific paths), or route job submission through a login-node request; until then, correct LIMITS.md and the peer note.
- **Could break when:** A Slurm upgrade moving client library/plugin paths breaks any bind added for this.
- **Code:** grid/ashleyd.js:303-306; docs/LIMITS.md:13-15,55; grid/ashley:32-37,310-316

### `container.image-contract` Container image provides what Ashley runs

Depends on: `grid.container`

- **Assumes:** Every library image has Node >= 18 (22) at /usr/local/bin/node, which bootstrap, adopt, is_ready and the worker template test for. It also has python3 for the publish merge, git, curl with CA certificates, bash, util-linux `script` for the phone pty, and a glibc above Claude Code's floor. docs/INSTALL.md's nvidia/cuda example has no /usr/local/bin/node, so its publish fails.

### `container.claude-version-supported` Pinned Claude Code still accepted

Depends on: `container.image-contract`, `grid.token-claude`

- **Assumes:** The pinned @anthropic-ai/claude-code@2.1.283 still authenticates and runs against the Anthropic API (no minimum-version refusal), and the version recorded in READY or library.json equals `claude --version`.
- **Check:** grid (in the worker): `claude --version` equals the recorded claude_version. A 1-turn `claude -p ok --max-turns 1` with the account's token returns is_error=false.

### `container.claude-headless-contract` claude -p flags and stream-json shape

The final line must be {type:'result', is_error, subtype, result, session_id, total_cost_usd, duration_ms, num_turns, usage}, and --bare must never be used.

The six auth and redirect variables are the complete set that can override the credential.

Depends on: `container.claude-version-supported`

- **Assumes:** Transcripts live at <CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR>/projects/<cwd with non-alphanumerics as ->/<uuid>.jsonl, and --resume searches only there (measured on 2.1.283). Claude Code follows the projects symlink to ~/.ashley/projects.
